## Motor Vehicle Safety Regulations {#motor-vehicle-safety-regulations .Title-of-Act}

[C.R.C.]{.abbr title="Consolidated Regulations of Canada"}, c. 1038
:::

[MOTOR VEHICLE SAFETY ACT](/eng/acts/M-10.01)

Motor Vehicle Safety Regulations
::::

::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/2020-22,
s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-1-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 1"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-478115 .Part}

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In these
  Regulations,

  [[AATCC]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[AATCC]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the American Association of
      Textile Chemists and Colorists; ([AATCC]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[accessory mass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[accessory mass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2008-258, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Motor Vehicle Safety
      Act](/eng/acts/M-10.01); ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[adjacent seat]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[adjacent seat]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a designated seating
      position so located that a portion of its occupant space is not
      more than 25.4 cm from an emergency exit for a distance of at
      least 38.1 cm measured horizontally and parallel to the emergency
      exit; ([siège adjacent]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[adjustment hardware]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[adjustment hardware]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means hardware
      designed for adjusting the size of a seat belt assembly to fit the
      user, including such hardware as may be integral with a buckle, a
      retractor or attachment hardware; ([pièces de
      réglage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[agricultural commodity trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[agricultural commodity trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/99-357, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[aiming reference plane]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[aiming reference plane]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/96-366, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[air bag]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[air bag]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an inflatable automatic
      occupant protection system that may be deployed when a collision
      occurs; ([sac gonflable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[air brake system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[air brake system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a brake system that
      uses air as a medium for transmitting pressure or force from the
      driver control to the service brake, including an
      air-over-hydraulic brake subsystem, but does not include a system
      that uses compressed air or vacuum only to assist the driver in
      applying muscular force to hydraulic or mechanical
      components; ([système de freinage à air comprimé]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[all-terrain vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[all-terrain vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a wheeled or
      tracked vehicle, other than a snowmobile or work vehicle, designed
      primarily for recreational use or for the transportation of
      property or equipment exclusively on undeveloped road rights of
      way, marshland, open country or other unprepared
      surfaces; ([véhicule tout terrain]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ambient temperature]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ambient temperature]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the surrounding
      air temperature measured at such a distance from a vehicle under
      test that the temperature is not significantly affected by heat
      from the vehicle; ([température ambiante]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[American compliance label]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[American compliance label]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a label
      referred to in section 30115, chapter 301, title 49 of the United
      States Code that is applied to a vehicle by a manufacturer that
      certifies that the vehicle conforms to the federal laws of the
      United States that are applicable on the date of
      manufacture; ([étiquette de conformité
      américaine]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[American specifications vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[American specifications
      vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/95-147, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[ANSI]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ANSI]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the American National Standards
      Institute; ([ANSI]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ANS Z26]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ANS Z26]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/94-717, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[ANSI Z26 Safety Code --- 1990]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ANSI Z26 Safety Code --- 1990]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      American National Standard for Safety Glazing Materials for
      Glazing Motor Vehicles and Motor Vehicle Equipment Operating on
      Land Highways --- Safety Code, Standard ANSI/SAE Z26.1 --- 1990,
      published November 1990 and reprinted February 1992; ([code de
      sécurité ANSI Z26 --- 1990]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ANSI Z26 Safety Standard --- 1996]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ANSI Z26 Safety Standard --- 1996]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      the American National Standard for Safety Glazing Materials for
      Glazing Motor Vehicles and Motor Vehicle Equipment Operating on
      Land Highways --- Safety Standard, Standard ANSI/SAE Z26.1-1996,
      published on August 11,1997; ([norme de sécurité ANSI Z26 ---
      1996]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[anthropomorphic test device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[anthropomorphic test device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, except
      in section 202 of Schedule IV, a representation of a human being
      used in the measurement of the conditions that a human being would
      experience in a vehicle when the vehicle is subjected to approved
      test methods; ([dispositif anthropomorphe
      d'essai]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[antilock brake system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[antilock brake system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or
      [[ABS]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the portion of a service brake
      system that automatically controls the degree of rotational wheel
      slip during braking by

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} sensing the rate of angular rotation of the
        wheels,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} transmitting signals regarding the rate of
        wheel angular rotation to one or more controlling devices that
        interpret those signals and generate responsive controlling
        output signals, and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} transmitting those controlling signals to one
        or more modulators that adjust brake actuating forces in
        response to those signals; ([dispositif de frein
        antiblocage]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"}*ou*[ABS]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} )

  [[antilock system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[antilock system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/97-200, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[antique reproduction vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[antique reproduction vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      vehicle that is designed to be a scaled reproduction of an antique
      vehicle and

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} may contain contemporary design components,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has a motor that produces 8 kW (10.73 bhp) or
        less,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is intended for use exclusively in parades,
        exhibitions and demonstrations, and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} bears a label, permanently affixed in a
        conspicuous position, stating that the vehicle is not to be used
        for public transportation, but is intended for use in parades,
        exhibitions and demonstrations; ([réplique d'ancien
        modèle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[antique vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[antique vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle more than
      30 years old that, when restored to a condition comparable to that
      on the date of its manufacture, retains the original components or
      incorporates replacement components with original design
      characteristics; ([ancien modèle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[approved]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[approved]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means approved by the
      Minister; ([approuvé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[armour]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[armour]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2007-180, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[assembler]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[assembler]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/95-147, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[ASTM]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ASTM]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the American Society for
      Testing Materials; ([ASTM]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[attachment hardware]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[attachment hardware]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the hardware
      designed for securing the webbing of a seat belt assembly to a
      vehicle; ([pièces de fixation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[automatic-locking retractor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[automatic-locking retractor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      retractor incorporating adjustment hardware that has a positive
      self-locking mechanism that is capable, when locked, of
      withstanding restraint forces; ([rétracteur
      autobloquant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[automatic occupant protection system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[automatic occupant protection system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      a protective restraining system for an occupant of a vehicle that
      does not require any deliberate action on the part of the occupant
      to be activated where that occupant enters, occupies or leaves the
      vehicle; ([dispositif automatique de protection de
      l'occupant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[auto transporter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[auto transporter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a truck and a
      trailer designed for use in combination to transport motor
      vehicles where the truck is designed to carry cargo other than at
      the fifth wheel and that cargo is to be loaded only by means of
      the trailer; ([porte-autos]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[back door]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[back door]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a door or door system on
      the back of a motor vehicle through which passengers can enter or
      leave the vehicle or cargo can be loaded or unloaded, but does not
      include

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a trunk lid, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a door or window that is composed entirely of
        glazing material and whose latches or hinge systems are attached
        directly to the glazing material; ([porte
        arrière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[backup system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[backup system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a portion of a service
      brake system, such as a pump, that automatically supplies energy
      in the event of a primary brake power source failure; ([système de
      secours]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[battery charging indicator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[battery charging indicator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a device
      showing whether the battery is being charged or
      discharged; ([indicateur de charge]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[battery charging tell-tale]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[battery charging tell-tale]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a signal
      that, when alight, indicates that the battery is not being
      charged; ([témoin de charge]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[beam contributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[beam contributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/96-366, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[blister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[blister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2007-180, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[body type]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[body type]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the general configuration
      or shape of a vehicle distinguished by the number of doors or
      windows, cargo carrying features, the roofline (e.g., sedan,
      fastback, hatchback) or other characteristics; ([type de
      carrosserie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[bonded construction sealed beam headlamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[bonded construction sealed beam
      headlamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/96-366, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[booster cushion]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[booster cushion]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/98-160, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[booster seat]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[booster seat]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 100(1) of the [Motor Vehicle Restraint Systems and
      Booster Seats Safety
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2010-90); ([siège
      d'appoint]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[brake fluid]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[brake fluid]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2007-180, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[brake hose]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[brake hose]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2007-180, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[brake hose assembly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[brake hose assembly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2007-180, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[brake hose end fitting]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[brake hose end fitting]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2007-180, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[brake power assist unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[brake power assist unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a device
      installed in a hydraulic brake system that reduces the amount of
      muscular force that a driver must apply to actuate the system, and
      that, if inoperative, does not prevent the driver from braking the
      vehicle by a continued application of muscular force on the
      service brake control; ([unité d'assistance de
      frein]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[brake power unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[brake power unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a device installed
      in a brake system that provides the energy required to actuate the
      brakes, either directly or indirectly, through an auxiliary
      device, with driver action consisting only of modulating the
      energy application level; ([unité de servo-frein]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[braking interval]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[braking interval]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the distance
      measured from the point of initiation of one brake application to
      the point of initiation of the next brake
      application; ([intervalle de freinage]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[buckle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[buckle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a quick-release connector
      that secures a person in a seat belt assembly or a built-in
      restraint system; ([attache]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[built-in booster cushion]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[built-in booster cushion]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2013-117, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[built-in booster seat]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[built-in booster seat]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a device that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is an integral part of a vehicle seat, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is designed to seat a person whose mass is at
        least 18 kg, to ensure that the seat belt assembly fits
        properly; ([siège d'appoint intégré]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[built-in child restraint system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[built-in child restraint
      system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2013-117, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[built-in dual-purpose restraint system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[built-in dual-purpose restraint
      system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2013-117, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[built-in restraint system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[built-in restraint system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a device
      that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is an integral part of a vehicle seat, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is designed to restrain only a person whose
        mass is more than 10 kg but not more than 41 kg; ([ensemble
        intégré de retenue]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[bus]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[bus]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle having a designated
      seating capacity of more than 10, but does not include a
      restricted-use vehicle or trailer; ([autobus]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[bus trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[bus trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle having a
      designated seating capacity of more than 10 and designed primarily
      to be drawn behind another
      vehicle; ([remorque-autobus]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[C-dolly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[C-dolly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a trailer converter dolly
      that is equipped with a single axle that is self-steering and with
      a coupling that is so designed that when the trailer converter
      dolly is coupled to a towing trailer, the trailer converter dolly
      cannot pivot horizontally with respect to the towing
      trailer; ([chariot de conversion de type C]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[cable reel trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[cable reel trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle
      designed to be drawn behind another vehicle for the exclusive
      purpose of carrying a drum or reel of cable; ([chariot
      dérouleur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[cargo-carrying capacity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[cargo-carrying capacity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the mass
      that is equal to or less than the result obtained by subtracting
      from the gross vehicle weight rating the sum of

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the unloaded vehicle mass,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the product obtained by multiplying the
        designated seating capacity by 54 kg, in the case of a school
        bus, or by 68 kg, in any other case, and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle having living or
        sanitary accommodations, the mass of its fresh water, hot water
        and propane tanks, but not its waste water tanks, when
        full; ([capacité de chargement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[chassis-cab]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[chassis-cab]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an incomplete vehicle,
      other than an incomplete trailer, with a completed occupant
      compartment that requires only the addition of a cargo-carrying
      surface, work-performing equipment or a load-bearing component to
      perform its intended functions; ([châssis-cabine]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[check digit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[check digit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a single number or the
      letter "X" used to verify the accuracy of the transcription of the
      vehicle identification number; ([unité de
      contrôle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[child]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[child]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person whose mass is more
      than 10 kg and not more than 30 kg; ([enfant]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[child restraint system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[child restraint system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/98-160, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[chipping]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[chipping]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2007-180, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[CMVSS]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CMVSS]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} is an abbreviation for Canada Motor
      Vehicle Safety Standard; ([NSVAC]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[competition car]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[competition car]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2000-182, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[competition motorcycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[competition motorcycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2000-182, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[competition snowmobile]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[competition snowmobile]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2000-182, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[competition vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[competition vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle that
      is designed for use exclusively in closed-course competition and

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} bears a label affixed by the manufacturer
        stating, in both official languages, that the vehicle is a
        competition vehicle and is for use exclusively in closed-course
        competition, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is accompanied by a signed declaration clearly
        indicating that the vehicle is a competition vehicle and is for
        use exclusively in closed-course competition; ([véhicule de
        compétition]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[completed vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[completed vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle that
      needs no further manufacturing operations to perform its intended
      function, other than the addition of readily attachable
      components, such as mirrors or tire and rim assemblies, or minor
      finishing operations such as painting; ([véhicule
      complet]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[compliance label]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[compliance label]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the label required
      to be applied to a completed vehicle under section 6 or
      6.6; ([étiquette de conformité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[compressed natural gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[compressed natural gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or
      [[CNG]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means natural gas, composed
      predominantly of methane, compressed at pressures up to 20 680 kPa
      (3,000 psi); ([gaz naturel comprimé]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"}*or*[GNC]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} )

  [[contactable surface]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[contactable surface]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/98-160, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[control]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[control]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the part of a device that
      enables the driver to bring about a change in the state or
      functioning of a vehicle or vehicle
      component; ([commande]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[convertible]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[convertible]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle that has an
      A-pillar or windshield peripheral support, the upper portion of
      which is not joined by a fixed rigid structure to the B-pillar or
      other rear roof support to the rear of the
      B-pillar; ([décapotable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CSA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CSA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Canadian Standards
      Association; ([CSA]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[curb mass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[curb mass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the mass of a vehicle with
      standard equipment and carrying its maximum capacity of fuel, oil
      and coolant and includes the mass of any air-conditioning
      equipment on the vehicle and the amount by which the mass of any
      optional engine with which the vehicle is equipped exceeds the
      mass of the standard engine; ([masse à vide]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[curb weight]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[curb weight]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the weight of a vehicle
      with standard equipment and carrying its maximum capacity of fuel,
      oil and coolant and includes the weight of any air-conditioning
      equipment on the vehicle and the amount by which the weight of any
      optional engine with which the vehicle is equipped exceeds the
      weight of the standard engine; ([poids à vide]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[CUSMA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CUSMA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned by the
      definition [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the
      [Canada--United States--Mexico Agreement Implementation
      Act](/eng/acts/C-10.65); ([ACEUM]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[custom restraint system for disabled persons]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[custom restraint system for disabled
      persons]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/98-160, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[cutaway chassis]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[cutaway chassis]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an incomplete
      vehicle that has the back of the cab cut out for the intended
      installation of a structure that permits access from the driver's
      area to the back of the vehicle; ([châssis
      tronqué]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[date of manufacture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[date of manufacture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the day, month
      and year on which the main assembly of the vehicle was
      completed; ([date de fabrication]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[daytime running lamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[daytime running lamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a lamp that
      produces a steady-burning light signal intended to improve the
      visibility of a vehicle from the front and the front sides; ([feu
      de jour]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[demountable rim]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[demountable rim]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a supporting member
      for a tire or tire and tube assembly, that does not have a
      permanently attached centre component; ([jante
      amovible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[designated seating capacity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[designated seating capacity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      designated seating capacity determined in accordance with section
      2.3; ([nombre désigné de places assises]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[designated seating position]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[designated seating position]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      location in a vehicle that is likely to be used as a seating
      position and that has a seating surface width of at least
      330 mm; ([place assise désignée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[disabled person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[disabled person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who, for
      orthopaedic reasons or because of the person's build or other
      physical characteristics, requires a vehicle that has been adapted
      to accommodate their disability; ([personne
      handicapée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[disc wheel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[disc wheel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a supporting member for a
      tire or tire and tube assembly, comprising a rim with a
      dish-shaped component that is permanently attached to the inner
      circumference of the rim; ([roue à disque]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[display]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[display]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, except in section 101 of
      Schedule IV, an indicator, a tell-tale or an alphanumeric readout,
      or a collection of indicators, tell-tales and alphanumeric
      readouts, on the instrument panel of a
      vehicle; ([affichage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[distributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[distributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/95-147, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[driver]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[driver]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the occupant of a vehicle
      seated immediately behind the steering control
      system; ([conducteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[driver-operated accelerator control system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[driver-operated accelerator control
      system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means all components of a vehicle,
      except the fuel metering device, that regulate engine speed in
      direct response to movement of the driver-operated control and
      that return the throttle to the idle position upon release of the
      driver-operated control; ([système de commande d'accélération
      actionné par le conducteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ECE]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ECE]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the United Nations Economic
      Commission for Europe, Inland Transport
      Committee; ([CEE]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[effective projected luminous lens area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[effective projected luminous lens
      area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/96-366, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[emergency brake]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[emergency brake]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a mechanism designed
      to stop a vehicle after a failure of the service brake
      system; ([frein de secours]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[emergency-locking retractor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[emergency-locking retractor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      retractor incorporating adjustment hardware that has a locking
      mechanism that is activated by vehicle acceleration, webbing
      movement in relation to the vehicle or other automatic action
      during an emergency, and is capable, when locked, of withstanding
      restraint forces; ([rétracteur à blocage
      d'urgence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[emergency vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[emergency vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any fire-fighting
      vehicle, ambulance, police vehicle or other vehicle that is used
      for the purpose of an emergency; ([véhicule de
      secours]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[enclosed motorcycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[enclosed motorcycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a motorcycle
      that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has steering handlebars that are completely
        constrained from rotating in relation to the axle of only one
        wheel in contact with the ground,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is designed to travel on two wheels in contact
        with the ground,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} has a minimum driver's seat height, when the
        vehicle is unladen, of 650 mm, and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} has a structure partially or fully enclosing
        the driver and passenger that is an integral part of the vehicle
        chassis; ([motocyclette à habitacle fermé]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[engine coolant temperature indicator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[engine coolant temperature indicator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      a device that presents information concerning the temperature of
      the coolant; ([indicateur de température du liquide de
      refroidissement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[engine coolant temperature tell-tale]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[engine coolant temperature tell-tale]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      a signal that, when alight, indicates that the temperature of the
      engine coolant is above the normal engine running temperature
      prescribed by the manufacturer; ([témoin de température du liquide
      de refroidissement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[engine type]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[engine type]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a power source
      distinguished by the fuel utilized, number of cylinders,
      displacement, net power or other characteristics; ([type de
      moteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ERBP]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ERBP]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2007-180, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[final-stage manufacturer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[final-stage manufacturer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a company
      that performs the manufacturing operations on an incomplete
      vehicle that turn the incomplete vehicle into a completed
      vehicle; ([fabricant à l'étape finale]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[fixed collision barrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fixed collision barrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a device
      that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} consists of

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a structure with a flat, vertical, unyielding
          impact surface that is of a size sufficient to ensure that no
          portion of a vehicle striking the surface projects or passes
          beyond the surface, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a horizontal approach surface that does not
          impede vehicle motion during impact and that is of a size
          sufficient to ensure that a vehicle will be able to attain a
          stable attitude during its approach to the impact surface, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} does not absorb any significant portion of the
        kinetic energy of a vehicle striking the impact
        surface; ([barrière fixe pour essais de
        collision]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[flash]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[flash]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a cycle of automatic
      activation and deactivation of a lamp that continues until stopped
      either automatically or manually; ([clignotement]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[forward control configuration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[forward control configuration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      configuration in which more than half of the engine length is
      rearward of the foremost point of the windshield base and the
      steering wheel hub is in the forward quarter of the vehicle
      length; ([type à cabine avancée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[free length]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[free length]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2007-180, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[front outboard designated seating position]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[front outboard designated seating
      position]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the driver's designated
      seating position and the forwardmost right outboard designated
      seating position, but does not include a school bus passenger
      designated seating position; ([place assise désignée extérieure
      avant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[fuel container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fuel container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means one or more fuel
      containers with integral valving, pressure relief devices, tubing,
      hoses and mounting brackets; ([réservoir de
      carburant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[fuel level indicator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fuel level indicator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a device that
      presents information concerning the amount of fuel in the
      tank; ([indicateur de niveau de carburant]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[fuel level tell-tale]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fuel level tell-tale]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a signal that,
      when alight, indicates that the fuel level is close to zero or
      that the vehicle is running on its fuel reserve; ([témoin de
      niveau de carburant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[fuel metering device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fuel metering device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the carburetor,
      fuel injector, fuel distributor or fuel injection
      pump; ([dispositif de dosage du carburant]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[fuel spillage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fuel spillage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the fall, flow or run
      of fuel from a vehicle but does not include wetness resulting from
      capillary action; ([écoulement de carburant]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[fuel system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fuel system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means all components used to
      store fuel or supply fuel to a vehicle engine; ([circuit
      d'alimentation en carburant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[full trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[full trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, for the purposes of
      Technical Standards Document No. 121, Air Brake Systems, means a
      trailer, except a pole trailer, that is equipped with two or more
      axles that support the entire weight of the trailer and its
      load; ([remorque complète]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[gasoline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[gasoline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2002-187, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[glazing material manufacturer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[glazing material manufacturer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      person engaged in the business of fabricating, laminating or
      tempering glazing material; ([fabricant de
      vitrages]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[grade]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[grade]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2007-180, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[gross axle weight rating]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[gross axle weight rating]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or
      [[GAWR]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the value specified by the
      vehicle manufacturer as the load-carrying capacity of a single
      axle system, as measured at the tire-ground interfaces; ([poids
      nominal brut sur l'essieu]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"}*or*[PNBE]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} )

  [[gross vehicle weight rating]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[gross vehicle weight rating]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or
      [[GVWR]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the value specified by the
      vehicle manufacturer as the loaded weight of a single
      vehicle; ([poids nominal brut du véhicule]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"}*or*[PNBV]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} )

  [[H-point]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[H-point]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the mechanically hinged hip
      point of a manikin that simulates the actual pivot centre of the
      human torso and thigh, described in SAE Standard J826, Devices for
      Use in Defining and Measuring Vehicle Seating Accommodation (July
      1995); ([point H]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[H-V axis]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[H-V axis]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2018-43, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[hardware]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[hardware]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, when used in relation to a seat
      belt assembly, means any metal or rigid plastic
      part; ([pièces]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[head impact area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[head impact area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the area described
      in subsection 201(1) of Schedule IV; ([zone d'impact de la
      tête]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[headlamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[headlamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a lighting device that
      produces an upper beam, a lower beam, or
      both; ([projecteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[headlamp assembly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[headlamp assembly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2018-43, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[head restraint]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[head restraint]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a device that limits
      rearward angular displacement of the occupant's head relative to
      their torso line; ([appuie-tête]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[hearse]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[hearse]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle that contains only
      one row of occupant seats, is designed exclusively for
      transporting a body and casket and is equipped with features to
      secure a casket in place during the operation of the
      vehicle; ([corbillard]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[heavy duty vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[heavy duty vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2001-35, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[heavy hauler trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[heavy hauler trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a trailer that
      has

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} brake lines designed to adapt to separation or
        extension of the vehicle frame, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a body that consists of only a platform the
        primary cargo-carrying surface of which is not more than
        101.6 cm (40 inches) above the ground in an unloaded condition,
        but may include sides that are designed for easy removal and a
        permanent front end structure; ([remorque
        lourde]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[high-pressure portion of the fuel system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[high-pressure portion of the fuel
      system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, for a vehicle that uses LPG or
      CNG as a source of energy for its propulsion, all the components
      of the fuel system from and including the fuel container to, but
      not including, the first stage regulator; ([partie haute pression
      du circuit d'alimentation en carburant]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[hub]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[hub]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a rotating member that provides
      for mounting of disc wheels; ([moyeu]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[hydraulic brake system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[hydraulic brake system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a system that
      uses hydraulic fluid as a medium for transmitting force from a
      service brake control to the service brake, and that may
      incorporate a brake power assist unit or a brake power
      unit; ([système de freinage hydraulique]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[hydraulic system mineral oil]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[hydraulic system mineral oil]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2007-180, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[idle position]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[idle position]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the position of the
      throttle at which it first comes in contact with an engine idle
      speed control appropriate for existing conditions according to the
      manufacturers' recommendations respecting engine speed adjustments
      for a cold engine, air conditioning, emission control and throttle
      setting devices; ([position de ralenti]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[imported used vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[imported used vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/92-173, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[importer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[importer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/95-147, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[incomplete trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[incomplete trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle that is
      capable of being drawn and that consists, at a minimum, of a
      chassis structure and suspension system but that needs further
      manufacturing operations to become a completed vehicle; ([remorque
      incomplète]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[incomplete vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[incomplete vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is capable of being driven and that
        consists, at a minimum, of a chassis structure, power train,
        steering system, suspension system and braking system in the
        state in which they are to be part of the completed vehicle, but
        requires further manufacturing operations to become a completed
        vehicle, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that is an incomplete trailer; ([véhicule
        incomplet]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[incomplete vehicle manufacturer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[incomplete vehicle manufacturer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      company that manufactures an incomplete vehicle by assembling
      components none of which, taken separately, constitutes an
      incomplete vehicle; ([fabricant de véhicules
      incomplets]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[indicator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[indicator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the part of an instrument
      that shows the quantity of the physical characteristic that the
      instrument is designed to sense; ([indicateur]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[infant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[infant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who is unable to
      walk unassisted and whose mass is not more than
      10 kg; ([bébé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[infant restraint system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[infant restraint system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/98-160, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[information label]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[information label]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the label required
      to be applied to an incomplete vehicle under section 6.2 or
      6.4; ([étiquette informative]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[information readout display]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[information readout display]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/93-31, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[initial brake temperature]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[initial brake temperature]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/99-357, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[inspector]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[inspector]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/95-147, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[integral headlamp aiming device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[integral headlamp aiming
      device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/96-366, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[intermediate manufacturer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[intermediate manufacturer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a company,
      other than an incomplete vehicle manufacturer or final-stage
      manufacturer, that performs manufacturing operations on an
      incomplete vehicle; ([fabricant intermédiaire]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[IRHD]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[IRHD]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2007-180, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[leaded gasoline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[leaded gasoline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means gasoline that
      contains more than

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 0.06 grams of lead per Imperial gallon (0.013
        grams per litre), or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 0.006 grams of phosphorous per Imperial gallon
        (0.0013 grams per litre); ([essence au plomb]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[light duty vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[light duty vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2001-35, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[lightly loaded vehicle weight]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[lightly loaded vehicle weight]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/99-357, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[light source]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[light source]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/96-366, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[light-truck tire]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[light-truck tire]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or [[LT
      tire]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a tire designated by its
      manufacturer as primarily intended for use on lightweight trucks
      or multi-purpose passenger vehicles; ([pneu pour camion
      léger]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[limited-speed motorcycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[limited-speed motorcycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      motorcycle that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has steering handlebars that are completely
        constrained from rotating in relation to the axle of only one
        wheel in contact with the ground,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has a maximum speed of 70 km/h or less,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} has a minimum driver's seat height, when the
        vehicle is unladen, of 650 mm, and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} does not have a structure partially or fully
        enclosing the driver and passenger, other than that part of the
        vehicle forward of the driver's torso and the seat
        backrest; ([motocyclette à vitesse limitée]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[line]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[line]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the name that a manufacturer
      applies to a family of vehicles within a make that have a degree
      of commonality of body, chassis, cab type or other features of
      construction; ([ligne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[liquefied petroleum gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[liquefied petroleum gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or
      [[LPG]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a hydrocarbon product that meets
      National Standard of Canada CAN/CGSB-3.14-M88, Liquefied Petroleum
      Gas (Propane) (August 1988). ([gaz de pétrole
      liquéfié]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"}*ou*[GPL]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"} )

  [[load divider dolly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[load divider dolly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a trailer that
      consists of a trailer chassis and one or more axles, with no solid
      bed, body or container attached, and that is designed exclusively
      to support a portion of the load on a trailer or truck excluded
      from all the requirements of Technical Standards Document No. 121,
      Air Brake Systems; ([chariot de répartition de
      charge]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[load-limiter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[load-limiter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a seat belt assembly
      component or seat belt assembly feature that controls tension on a
      seat belt to modulate the forces that are imparted to an occupant
      who is restrained by the seat belt assembly during a
      collision; ([limiteur de charge]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[lower beam]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[lower beam]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a beam that is intended
      to illuminate the road and its environs ahead of a vehicle when
      the vehicle is meeting or closely following another
      vehicle; ([faisceau de croisement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[lower connector system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[lower connector system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2013-117, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[lower universal anchorage system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[lower universal anchorage system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      device, other than a vehicle seat belt, that is designed to secure
      the lower portion of a restraint system or booster seat to a
      vehicle and that transfers the load from the restraint system or
      booster seat and its occupant to the vehicle structure or a
      vehicle seat structure; ([dispositif universel d'ancrages
      d'attaches inférieurs]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[low-speed vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[low-speed vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle, other
      than a restricted-use vehicle, that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is designed for use primarily on streets and
        roads where access and the use of other prescribed classes of
        vehicles are controlled by law or agreement,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} travels on four wheels,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is powered by an electric power train (an
        electric motor and, if present, a transmission) that is designed
        to allow the vehicle to attain a speed of 32 km/h but not more
        than 40 km/h in a distance of 1.6 km on a paved level surface,

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} does not use fuel as an on-board source of
        energy, and

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} has a GVWR of less than 1 361 kg; ([véhicule à
        basse vitesse]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[make]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[make]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the name that a manufacturer
      applies to a group of vehicles; ([marque]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[manual seat belt assembly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[manual seat belt assembly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, with
      respect to a Type 1 or Type 2 seat belt assembly, an assembly that
      requires a deliberate action on the part of the occupant of a
      vehicle to be activated; ([ceinture de sécurité
      manuelle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[manufacturer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[manufacturer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/95-147, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[master lighting switch]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[master lighting switch]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a switch with
      one or more operational positions that controls the tail lamps,
      parking lamp, licence plate lamp, side marker lamps and headlamps
      and may control identification lamps and clearance
      lamps; ([commutateur général d'éclairage]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[maximum load]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[maximum load]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2008-258, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[maximum loaded vehicle mass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[maximum loaded vehicle mass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2008-258, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[maximum speed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[maximum speed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, with respect to a
      motorcycle, the speed specified by the manufacturer as the highest
      speed that the motorcycle is capable of attaining, measured in
      accordance with section 5.3; ([vitesse maximale]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[minibike]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[minibike]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/88-268, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of
      Transport; ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[mobile home]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[mobile home]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle that is more
      than 102 inches in overall width and that is designed to be drawn
      behind another vehicle and to be used as a living or working
      accommodation unit; ([maison roulante]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[mobility-impaired occupant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[mobility-impaired occupant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2013-117, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[model]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[model]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the name that a manufacturer
      applies to a family of vehicles of the same prescribed class,
      make, line, series and body type; ([modèle]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[model year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[model year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the year used to
      designate a discrete vehicle model irrespective of the calendar
      year in which the vehicle was actually produced, so long as the
      period of such production is less than two years; ([année de
      modèle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[moped]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[moped]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/88-268, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[motorcycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[motorcycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle, other than a
      restricted-use vehicle, passenger car, truck, multi-purpose
      passenger vehicle, competition vehicle or three-wheeled vehicle,
      that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is an enclosed motorcycle, limited-speed
        motorcycle, open motorcycle or motor tricycle,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is designed to travel on not more than three
        wheels in contact with the ground,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} has a minimum wheel rim diameter of 250 mm, and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} has a minimum wheelbase of 1
        016 mm; ([motocyclette]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[motor driven cycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[motor driven cycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/88-268, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[motor home]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[motor home]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a multi-purpose passenger
      vehicle that is designed to provide temporary residential
      accommodations, as evidenced by the presence of at least four of
      the following:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} cooking facilities,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a refrigerator or ice box,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a self-contained toilet,

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a heating or air-conditioning system that can
        function independently of the vehicle engine,

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a potable water supply system that includes a
        faucet and sink, and

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a separate 110- to 125-V electric power supply
        or an LP gas supply; ([autocaravane]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[motor tricycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[motor tricycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a motorcycle, other
      than an antique reproduction vehicle, that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is designed to travel on three wheels that are
        in contact with the ground and symmetrically arranged in
        relation to the longitudinal median plane,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has seating on which the driver and passenger
        must sit astride,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} has not more than four designated seating
        positions,

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} has a GVWR of 1 000 kg or less,

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} has a maximum speed of more than 70 km/h, and

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} does not have a structure partially or fully
        enclosing the driver and passenger, other than that part of the
        vehicle forward of the driver's torso and the seat
        backrest; ([tricycle à moteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Motor Vehicle Safety Test Methods]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Motor Vehicle Safety Test
      Methods]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/97-141, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Motor Vehicle Safety Test Methods, section 106, "Brake Hoses"]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Motor Vehicle Safety Test Methods, section 106, "Brake
      Hoses"]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2007-180, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Motor Vehicle Safety Test Methods, section 116, "Hydraulic Brake Fluid"]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Motor Vehicle Safety Test Methods, section 116, "Hydraulic Brake
      Fluid"]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/95-536, s.
      7\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Motor Vehicle Safety Test Methods, section 116, "Hydraulic Brake Fluids"]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Motor Vehicle Safety Test Methods, section 116, "Hydraulic Brake
      Fluids"]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2007-180, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[multifunction school activity bus]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[multifunction school activity bus]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      school bus that is designed to pick up and drop off students under
      circumstances in which there is no need to control
      traffic; ([autobus multifonction pour les activités
      scolaires]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[multiple compartment lamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[multiple compartment lamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/96-366, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[multiple lamp arrangement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[multiple lamp arrangement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/96-366, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[multi-purpose passenger vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[multi-purpose passenger vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      vehicle having a designated seating capacity of 10 or less that is
      constructed either on a truck chassis or with special features for
      occasional off-road operation, but does not include an air cushion
      vehicle, all-terrain vehicle, low-speed vehicle, passenger car,
      restricted-use vehicle, three-wheeled vehicle or truck; ([véhicule
      de tourisme à usages multiples]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[non-locking retractor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[non-locking retractor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a retractor
      that does not have a locking mechanism, from which the webbing can
      be extended to substantially its full length by a small external
      force, that provides no adjustment for assembly length and that
      need not be capable of sustaining restraint forces at maximum
      webbing extension; ([rétracteur sans blocage]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[normal load]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[normal load]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2008-258, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[normal occupants' mass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[normal occupants' mass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2008-258, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[occupant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[occupant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person or manikin seated
      in a vehicle and, unless otherwise specified, means a person or
      manikin having the dimensions and weight of a 95th percentile
      adult male; ([occupant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[occupant compartment air space]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[occupant compartment air space]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2007-180, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[occupant distribution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[occupant distribution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2008-258, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[occupant space]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[occupant space]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the space directly
      above the seat and footwell, bounded vertically by the ceiling and
      horizontally by the normally positioned seat back and the nearest
      obstruction of occupant motion in the direction the seat
      faces; ([espace d'occupant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[off-road motorcycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[off-road motorcycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/88-268, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[oil pressure indicator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[oil pressure indicator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a device that
      presents information concerning the pressure of the oil in the
      engine lubrication circuit; ([indicateur de pression
      d'huile]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[oil pressure tell-tale]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[oil pressure tell-tale]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a signal
      that, when alight, indicates that the oil pressure in the engine
      lubrication circuit is below the normal operating limit prescribed
      by the manufacturer; ([témoin de pression
      d'huile]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[on-highway vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[on-highway vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2004-250, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[on-off-highway vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[on-off-highway vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2004-250, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[open-body type vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[open-body type vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle
      that has no top over the occupant compartment or that has a top
      over the occupant compartment that can be installed or removed by
      the operator of the vehicle; ([véhicule de type
      ouvert]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[open motorcycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[open motorcycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a motorcycle that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has steering handlebars that are completely
        constrained from rotating in relation to the axle of only one
        wheel in contact with the ground,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is designed to travel on two wheels in contact
        with the ground or, if the wheels are asymmetrically arranged in
        relation to the longitudinal median plane, three wheels in
        contact with the ground,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} has a minimum driver's seat height, when the
        vehicle is unladen, of 650 mm,

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} has a maximum speed of more than 70 km/h, and

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} does not have a structure partially or fully
        enclosing the driver and passenger, other than that part of the
        vehicle forward of the driver's torso and the seat
        backrest; ([motocyclette sans habitacle fermé]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[optically combined lamps]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[optically combined lamps]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2018-43, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[optional item]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[optional item]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2008-258, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[outboard designated seating position]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[outboard designated seating position]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      a designated seating position where a longitudinal vertical plane
      tangent to the outboard side of the seat cushion is less than
      305 mm from the innermost point on the inside surface of the
      vehicle, which point is located vertically between the seating
      reference point and the shoulder reference point and
      longitudinally between the front and rear edges of the seat
      cushion; ([place assise désignée extérieure]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[overall width]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[overall width]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, except in sections
      104 and 108 of Schedule IV, the widest part of a vehicle with the
      doors and windows closed and the wheels in the straight-ahead
      position, exclusive of signal lamps, marker lamps, outside
      rearview mirrors, flexible fender extensions and mud
      flaps; ([largeur hors tout]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[parking brake]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[parking brake]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a mechanism designed
      to prevent the movement of a stationary vehicle; ([frein de
      stationnement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[parking mechanism]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[parking mechanism]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/99-357, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[passenger car]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[passenger car]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle having a
      designated seating capacity of 10 or less, but does not include an
      all-terrain vehicle, competition vehicle, low-speed vehicle,
      multi-purpose passenger vehicle, antique reproduction vehicle,
      motorcycle, restricted-use vehicle, truck, trailer or
      three-wheeled vehicle; ([voiture de tourisme]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[passenger car tire]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[passenger car tire]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a tire intended
      for use on passenger cars, multi-purpose passenger vehicles and
      trucks with a GVWR of 4 536 kg or less; ([pneu pour voiture de
      tourisme]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[passive occupant protection]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[passive occupant protection]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/93-5, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[passive restraint system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[passive restraint system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/93-5, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[pelvic restraint]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[pelvic restraint]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a seat belt
      assembly or portion thereof intended to restrain movement of the
      pelvis; ([ceinture sous-abdominale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[perimeter-seating bus]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[perimeter-seating bus]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a bus with
      seven or fewer designated seating positions rearward of the
      driver's designated seating position that are forward-facing or
      that can be adjusted to change the direction they are facing to
      forward-facing without the use of tools; ([autobus muni de sièges
      de périmètre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[permanently attached hose end fitting]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[permanently attached hose end
      fitting]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2007-180, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[plant of manufacture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[plant of manufacture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the plant at
      which the manufacturer affixes the vehicle identification
      number; ([usine de construction]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[pole trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[pole trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle designed to
      be drawn behind another vehicle by means of a reach or pole, or by
      being boomed or otherwise secured to the towing vehicle, for the
      purpose of transporting poles, pipes, structural members or other
      long or irregularly shaped loads capable generally of sustaining
      themselves as beams between the supporting connections; ([remorque
      pour charges longues]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[power-assisted bicycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[power-assisted bicycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2020-22, s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

  [[power-operated roof panel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[power-operated roof panel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2007-180, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[power-operated roof panel system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[power-operated roof panel system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      panel or panels in the roof of a vehicle that move on slides or
      hinges, the opening or closing of which is operated by a power
      source within the vehicle, but does not include a convertible top
      system; ([système de toit ouvrant à commande
      électrique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[prescribed class]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[prescribed class]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2020-22, s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

  [[prime glazing material manufacturer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[prime glazing material
      manufacturer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2002-187, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[prison bus]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[prison bus]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a bus that is specially
      designed for the purpose of transporting inmates; ([autobus
      pénitentiaire)]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"}

  [[production options mass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[production options mass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2008-258, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[production restraint system for disabled persons]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[production restraint system for disabled
      persons]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/98-160, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[pulpwood trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[pulpwood trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/99-357, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[push-out window]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[push-out window]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle window
      designed to open outward to provide for emergency
      egress; ([fenêtre basculante]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[readily removable window]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[readily removable window]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a window
      that can be quickly and completely removed from a vehicle without
      tools and, in the case of a bus having a GVWR of more than
      4 535.9 kg (10,000 pounds), shall include a push-out window and a
      window mounted in an emergency exit that can be manually pushed
      out of its location in the vehicle without the use of tools,
      regardless of whether the window remains hinged at one side to the
      vehicle; ([fenêtre amovible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[rear outboard designated seating position]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[rear outboard designated seating
      position]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any outboard designated
      seating position that is to the rear of a front outboard
      designated seating position, but does not include a designated
      seating position adjacent to a walkway located between the seat
      and the side of the vehicle interior that is designed to allow
      access to more rearward seating positions; ([place assise désignée
      extérieure arrière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[recreational trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[recreational trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a trailer
      designed to provide temporary living accommodation for travel,
      vacation or recreational use; ([remorque de
      camping]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[reflex reflector]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[reflex reflector]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a device on a
      vehicle that is intended to indicate the position and dimensions
      of the vehicle to the driver of an approaching vehicle using light
      reflected from the lamps of the approaching
      vehicle; ([cataphote]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[replaceable bulb headlamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[replaceable bulb headlamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/96-366, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[restraint system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[restraint system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      in subsection 100(1) of the [Motor Vehicle Restraint Systems and
      Booster Seats Safety
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2010-90); ([ensemble de
      retenue]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[restraint system for disabled persons]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[restraint system for disabled
      persons]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/98-160, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[restricted-use motorcycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[restricted-use motorcycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2020-22, s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

  [[restricted-use vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[restricted-use vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle ---
      excluding a competition vehicle but including an all-terrain
      vehicle designed primarily for recreational use --- that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is designed to travel on not more than four
        wheels in contact with the ground, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is not designed for use on public
        roads; ([véhicule à usage restreint]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[retractor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[retractor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a device for storing part
      or all of the webbing in a seat belt
      assembly; ([rétracteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[rim base]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[rim base]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2008-258, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[rim diameter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[rim diameter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the nominal diameter of
      the bead seat; ([diamètre de jante]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[rim size designation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[rim size designation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2008-258, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[rim type designation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[rim type designation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2008-258, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[rim width]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[rim width]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2008-258, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[rupture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[rupture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2007-180, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[SAE]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[SAE]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Society of Automotive
      Engineers, Inc. (SAE International); ([SAE]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[SAE Compatibility Fluid]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[SAE Compatibility Fluid]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2007-180, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[school bus]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[school bus]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a bus designed or
      equipped primarily to carry students to and from school or to and
      from school-related events; ([autobus scolaire]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[scuffing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[scuffing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2007-180, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[sealed beam headlamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[sealed beam headlamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2018-43, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[seat anchorage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[seat anchorage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any component that
      transfers a vehicle seat load to the vehicle structure; ([ancrage
      du siège]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[seat back retainer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[seat back retainer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2007-180, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[seat belt anchorage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[seat belt anchorage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any component of
      a vehicle, other than the webbing or straps, involved in
      transferring seat belt loads to the vehicle structure, including
      the attachment hardware, seat frames, seat pedestals, the vehicle
      structure and any part of the vehicle whose failure causes
      separation of the belt from the vehicle structure; ([ancrage de
      ceinture de sécurité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[seat belt assembly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[seat belt assembly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any strap,
      webbing or similar device designed to secure a person in a vehicle
      in order to mitigate the results of any accident, and includes all
      necessary buckles and other fasteners and all attachment hardware
      but does not include any strap, webbing or similar device that is
      part of a built-in restraint system; ([ceinture de
      sécurité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[seating reference point]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[seating reference point]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the unique
      Design H-Point, as defined in section 3.11.1 of SAE Recommended
      Practice J1100, Motor Vehicle Dimensions (February 2001), that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} establishes the rearmost normal design driving
        or riding position of each designated seating position, taking
        into account all modes of adjustment --- horizontal, vertical
        and tilt --- in a vehicle,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has X, Y and Z coordinates, as defined in
        section 3.3 of SAE Recommended Practice J1100, Motor Vehicle
        Dimensions (February 2001), established relative to the designed
        vehicle structure,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} simulates the position of the pivot centre of
        the human torso and thigh, and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} is the reference point employed to position the
        H-Point template with the 95th percentile leg, as described in
        section 4.1 of SAE Standard J826, Devices for Use in Defining
        and Measuring Vehicle Seating Accommodation (July 1995), or, if
        that template cannot be positioned, the reference point when the
        seat is in its rearmost adjustment position; ([point de
        référence de position assise]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[seating surface width]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[seating surface width]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the maximum
      width of a seating surface when it is measured in a zone extending
      from a transverse vertical plane that is 150 mm behind the front
      leading surface of that seating surface to a transverse vertical
      plane that is 250 mm behind that front leading surface, measured
      horizontally and longitudinally; ([largeur de la surface de
      siège]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[seat orientation reference line (SORL)]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[seat orientation reference line
      (SORL)]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2013-117, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[semi-trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[semi-trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a trailer constructed
      in such a manner that a substantial part of its weight rests upon
      or is carried by another vehicle by means of a fifth-wheel or
      similar coupling, but does not include a pole trailer, or any
      trailer designed to be drawn behind a passenger car or
      multi-purpose passenger vehicle; ([semi-remorque]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[series]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[series]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the name that a manufacturer
      applies to a subdivision of a line denoting the price, size or
      weight identification and that is utilized by the manufacturer for
      marketing purposes; ([série]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[service brake]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[service brake]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the primary mechanism
      designed to stop a vehicle; ([frein de service]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[shoulder reference point]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[shoulder reference point]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the point
      563 mm above the H-point along the torso line; ([point de
      référence de l'épaule]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[sloughing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[sloughing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2007-180, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[snowmobile]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[snowmobile]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle --- excluding a
      competition vehicle but including a snowmobile conversion vehicle
      --- that has a mass of not more than 450 kg, is designed primarily
      for travel on snow, has one or more steering skis and is driven by
      means of an endless belt or belts in contact with the
      ground; ([motoneige]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[snowmobile conversion vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[snowmobile conversion vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      vehicle designed to be capable of conversion to a snowmobile by
      the repositioning or addition of parts; ([véhicule convertible en
      motoneige]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})
