  [[snowmobile cutter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[snowmobile cutter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2016-318, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[snowmobile trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[snowmobile trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a trailer
      designed primarily for the transportation of
      snowmobiles; ([remorque pour motoneige]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[snub]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[snub]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the braking deceleration of a
      vehicle from a higher reference speed to a lower reference speed
      that is greater than zero; ([ralentissement]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[special driver accommodation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[special driver accommodation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a
      driver's seat that is designed to be removable or that has
      extended adjustment capability to allow a person to transfer from
      a wheelchair to the driver's seat; ([place du conducteur
      particulière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[speed attainable in 1.6 km (1 mile)]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[speed attainable in 1.6 km (1 mile)]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      the speed attainable by accelerating at maximum rate from a
      standing start for 1.6 km on a level surface; ([vitesse à 1,6 km
      (1 mille)]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[speed attainable in 3.2 km (2 miles)]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[speed attainable in 3.2 km (2 miles)]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      the speed attainable by accelerating at maximum rate from a
      standing start for 3.2 km on a level surface; ([vitesse à 3,2 km
      (2 milles)]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[spike stop]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[spike stop]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a stop resulting from the
      application of 889.6 N (200 pounds) of force on the service brake
      control in 0.08 second; ([arrêt d'urgence]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[split service brake system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[split service brake system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a brake
      system consisting of two or more subsystems actuated by a single
      control, designed so that a single failure in any subsystem (such
      as a leakage-type failure of a pressure component of a hydraulic
      subsystem, except for the structural failure of a housing that is
      common to two or more subsystems, or an electrical failure in an
      electrical subsystem) does not impair the operation of any other
      subsystem; ([système de frein de service partagé]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[spoke wheel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[spoke wheel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a rotating member that
      provides for mounting and support of demountable rims; ([roue à
      rayons]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[steering column]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[steering column]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the structural
      housing that surrounds a steering shaft; ([colonne de
      direction]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[steering control system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[steering control system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the basic
      steering mechanism and its associated trim hardware including any
      portion of a steering column assembly that provides energy
      absorption upon impact; ([système de commande de la
      direction]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[steering shaft]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[steering shaft]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a component that
      transmits steering torque from the steering wheel to the steering
      gear; ([arbre de direction]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[stickiness]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[stickiness]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2007-180, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[stopping distance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[stopping distance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the distance
      travelled by a vehicle from the point at which force is applied to
      the brake control to the point at which the vehicle reaches a full
      stop; ([distance d'arrêt]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[strap]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[strap]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a narrow band of non-woven
      material used in a seat belt assembly in place of
      webbing; ([courroie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[suspension spring]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[suspension spring]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a leaf, coil,
      torsion bar, rubber, air bag, and every other type of spring used
      in vehicular suspensions; ([ressort de
      suspension]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[tell-tale]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[tell-tale]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an optical signal that,
      when alight, indicates the activation or deactivation of a device,
      its correct or defective functioning or condition, or its failure
      to function; ([témoin]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[tether belt hook]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[tether belt hook]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/98-457, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[tether strap]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[tether strap]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a device that is fitted
      with a tether strap hook and secured to the rigid structure of a
      restraint system or booster seat, and that transfers the load from
      the restraint system or booster seat and its occupant to the
      user-ready tether anchorage; ([courroie
      d'attache]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[tether strap hook]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[tether strap hook]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a device that is
      used to attach a tether strap to a user-ready tether anchorage and
      that has an interface profile shown in Figure 1 of Schedule 7 to
      the [Motor Vehicle Restraint Systems and Booster Seats Safety
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2010-90) or, in the case of a
      device with integrated adjustment hardware, in Figure 2 of
      Schedule 7 to those Regulations; ([crochet de la courroie
      d'attache]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[three-wheeled vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[three-wheeled vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle,
      other than a competition vehicle, antique reproduction vehicle,
      motorcycle, restricted-use vehicle or trailer, that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is designed to travel on three wheels in
        contact with the ground,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has no more than four designated seating
        positions, and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} has a GVWR of 1 000 kg or less; ([véhicule à
        trois roues]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[throttle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[throttle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the component of the fuel
      metering device that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} connects to the driver-operated accelerator
        control system, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} controls the engine speed; ([papillon des
        gaz]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[torso line]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[torso line]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the line connecting the
      H-point and the shoulder reference point, described in SAE
      Standard J826, Devices for Use in Defining and Measuring Vehicle
      Seating Accommodation (July 1995); ([ligne de
      torse]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[trailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle designed to carry
      or accommodate persons or property and to be drawn behind another
      vehicle, and includes a bus trailer, pole trailer and cable reel
      trailer, but does not include earth-moving equipment or a mobile
      home, trailer converter dolly or implement of farm
      husbandry; ([remorque]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[trailer converter dolly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[trailer converter dolly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a conversion
      chassis that is equipped with one or more axles, the lower half of
      a fifth-wheel coupling and one or two drawbars; ([chariot de
      conversion]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[transit bus]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[transit bus]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a bus that is specially
      designed with space for standing passengers and that is equipped
      with a stop-request system; ([autobus urbain]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[transparent cover]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[transparent cover]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/96-366, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[truck]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[truck]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle designed primarily
      for the transportation of property or special-purpose equipment,
      but does not include a competition vehicle, crawler-mounted
      vehicle, three-wheeled vehicle, trailer, work vehicle, vehicle
      designed for operation exclusively off-road or low-speed
      vehicle; ([camion]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[truck tractor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[truck tractor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a truck designed
      primarily for drawing other vehicles and not constructed for
      carrying any load other than a part of the weight of the vehicle
      and load drawn, and includes a vehicle designed to accept a
      fifth-wheel coupling but does not include a crane-equipped
      breakdown vehicle; ([camion-tracteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Type 1]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Type 1]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2007-180, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[type 1 headlamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[type 1 headlamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/91-692, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Type 1 seat belt assembly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Type 1 seat belt assembly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a pelvic
      restraint; ([ceinture de sécurité de type 1]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Type 2]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Type 2]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2007-180, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[type 2 headlamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[type 2 headlamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/91-692, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Type 2 seat belt assembly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Type 2 seat belt assembly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      combination pelvic and upper torso restraint; ([ceinture de
      sécurité de type 2]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Type 2A]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Type 2A]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2007-180, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Type 2A shoulder belt]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Type 2A shoulder belt]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2013-9, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[unit magnification mirror]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[unit magnification mirror]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a plane or
      flat mirror with a reflective surface through which the angular
      height and width of the image of an object is equal to the angular
      height and width of the object when viewed directly at the same
      distance except for flaws that do not exceed normal manufacturing
      tolerances and includes a prismatic day-night adjustment rearview
      mirror that provides unit magnification in one of its
      positions; ([miroir plan]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[unleaded gasoline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[unleaded gasoline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means gasoline that
      contains not more than

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 0.06 grams of lead per Imperial gallon (0.013
        grams per litre), or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 0.006 grams of phosphorous per Imperial gallon
        (0.0013 grams per litre); ([essence sans plomb]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[unloaded vehicle mass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[unloaded vehicle mass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the mass of a
      vehicle equipped with the containers for the fluids necessary for
      the operation of the vehicle filled to their maximum capacity but
      without cargo or occupants; ([masse du véhicule sans
      charge]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[unloaded vehicle weight]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[unloaded vehicle weight]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the weight
      of a vehicle equipped with the containers for the fluids necessary
      for the operation of the vehicle filled to their maximum capacity,
      but without cargo or occupants; ([poids du véhicule sans
      charge]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[upper beam]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[upper beam]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a beam that is intended
      primarily for distance illumination ahead of a vehicle when the
      vehicle is not meeting or closely following another
      vehicle; ([faisceau de route]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[upper torso restraint]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[upper torso restraint]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a portion of a
      seat belt assembly intended to restrain movement of the chest and
      shoulder regions; ([ceinture-baudrier]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[used vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[used vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/91-425, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[user-ready tether anchorage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[user-ready tether anchorage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a device
      that transfers the tether strap load from a restraint system or
      booster seat and its occupant to the vehicle structure or a
      vehicle seat structure and that is designed to accept a tether
      strap hook directly, without requiring the installation of any
      other device; ([ancrage d'attache prêt à
      utiliser]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[vacuum tubing connector]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[vacuum tubing connector]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2007-180, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[variable brake proportioning system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[variable brake proportioning system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      a system that has one or more proportioning devices that
      automatically change the brake pressure ratio between any two or
      more wheels to compensate for changes in wheel loading resulting
      from static load changes or dynamic weight transfer, or from
      deceleration; ([compensateur de freinage]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[variable proportioning brake system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[variable proportioning brake
      system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/97-200, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/95-536, s.
      7\]]{.Repealed}

  [[vehicle capacity mass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[vehicle capacity mass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2008-258, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[vehicle fuel tank capacity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[vehicle fuel tank capacity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the volume of fuel left at the bottom of the
        tank when the fuel pump of the vehicle can no longer draw fuel
        from the tank

      plus

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the volume of fuel that can be pumped into the
        tank through the filler pipe when the vehicle is on a level
        surface and the volume of fuel referred to in paragraph (a) is
        already in the tank,

      except that the volume of fuel referred to in paragraph (b) does
      not include any volume of fuel that can be pumped into the fuel
      tank filler neck or into the space above the fuel tank filler
      neck; ([capacité du réservoir de carburant du
      véhicule]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[vehicle identification number]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[vehicle identification number]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      number consisting of arabic numerals, roman letters, or both that
      the manufacturer assigns to the vehicle for identification
      purposes; ([numéro d'identification du véhicule]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[vehicle imported temporarily for special purposes]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[vehicle imported temporarily for special
      purposes]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle imported for a
      purpose prescribed in section 11.1; ([véhicule importé
      temporairement à des fins spéciales]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[vehicle manufactured for operation by persons with disabilities]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[vehicle manufactured for operation by persons with
      disabilities]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2013-9, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[walk-in van]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[walk-in van]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a van type of truck in
      which a person having a height of 1 700 mm can enter the occupant
      compartment in an upright position by a front door; ([fourgon à
      accès en position debout]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[weather side]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[weather side]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2008-258, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[webbing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[webbing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a narrow band of fabric
      woven with continuous filling yarns and finished
      selvages; ([sangle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[wet ERBP]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[wet ERBP]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2007-180, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[wheelchair location]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[wheelchair location]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a location in a
      vehicle that is designed to be used to secure an occupied
      wheelchair; ([emplacement pour fauteuil roulant]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[work vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[work vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle designed
      primarily for the performance of work in the construction of works
      of civil engineering and in maintenance, that is not constructed
      on a truck-chassis or truck-type chassis, but does not include a
      tractor or any vehicle designed primarily to be drawn behind
      another vehicle; ([véhicule de travail]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[5th percentile adult female]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[5th percentile adult female]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person
      having as physical characteristics a mass of 46.3 kg, height of
      1499 mm, erect sitting height of 785 mm, normal sitting height of
      752 mm, hip sitting breadth of 325 mm, hip sitting circumference
      of 925 mm, waist sitting circumference of 599 mm, chest depth of
      191 mm, bust circumference of 775 mm, chest upper circumference of
      757 mm, chest lower circumference of 676 mm, knee height of
      455 mm, popliteal height of 356 mm, elbow rest height of 180 mm,
      thigh clearance height of 104 mm, buttock-to-knee length of
      518 mm, buttock-to-poples length of 432 mm, elbow-to-elbow breadth
      of 312 mm and seat breadth of 312 mm; ([5^e^ percentile adulte du
      sexe féminin]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[50th percentile adult male]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[50th percentile adult male]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person
      having as physical characteristics a mass of 74.4 kg plus or minus
      1.4 kg, erect sitting height of 907 mm plus or minus 3 mm, hip
      sitting breadth of 373 mm plus or minus 18 mm, hip sitting
      circumference of 1067 mm, waist sitting circumference of 813 mm
      plus or minus 15 mm, chest depth of 236 mm plus or minus 5 mm and
      chest circumference of 950 mm plus or minus 15 mm; ([50^e^
      percentile adulte du sexe masculin]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[50th percentile six-year-old child]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[50th percentile six-year-old child]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      person having as physical characteristics a mass of 21.5 kg, erect
      sitting height of 645 mm, hip sitting breadth of 213 mm, hip
      sitting circumference of 607 mm and waist sitting circumference of
      528 mm; ([50^e^ percentile enfant de six ans]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[95th percentile adult male]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[95th percentile adult male]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person
      having as physical characteristics a mass of 97.5 kg, height of
      1849 mm, erect sitting height of 965 mm, normal sitting height of
      930 mm, hip sitting breadth of 419 mm, hip sitting circumference
      of 1199 mm, waist sitting circumference of 1080 mm, chest depth of
      267 mm, chest circumference of 1130 mm, knee height of 594 mm,
      popliteal height of 490 mm, elbow rest height of 295 mm, thigh
      clearance height of 175 mm, buttock-to-knee length of 640 mm,
      buttock-to-poples length of 549 mm, elbow-to-elbow breadth of
      506 mm and seat breadth of 404 mm. ([95^e^ percentile adulte du
      sexe masculin]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In these Regulations, if a document that is available
  in both official languages is incorporated by reference as amended
  from time to time, an amendment to one language version of that
  document is not incorporated until the corresponding amendment is made
  to the other language version.

## [Metric or Imperial System]{.HTitleText1} {#h-478539 .Part}

**[[2.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.1 .sectionLabel}** If, in the application
to a vehicle of a portion of a section of these Regulations or a portion
of a provision of a technical standards document, either the metric or
the imperial system of measurement is used, the same system of
measurement shall be used in the application to the vehicle of any other
portion of the section or provision.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-263, s. 1
- SOR/82-482, s. 1
- SOR/96-366, s. 2
- SOR/2009-318, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-2.1-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 2.1"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Number of Wheels]{.HTitleText1} {#h-478543 .Part}

**[[2.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.2 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
determining the number of wheels on a motorcycle or a three-wheeled
vehicle, two wheels are considered to be one wheel if they are mounted
on the same axle and the distance between the centres of their areas of
contact with the ground is less than 460 mm.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2003-272, s. 2
- SOR/2009-318, s. 2
- SOR/2011-264, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-2.2-20091209.html "Link to previous version of section 2.2"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Designated Seating Capacity]{.HTitleText1} {#h-478547 .Part}

- **[[2.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and (3),
  the designated seating capacity of a vehicle is the sum of the number
  of designated seating positions and wheelchair locations in the
  vehicle.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The designated seating capacity of a motor home that
  has a GVWR greater than 4 536 kg may, at the option of the
  manufacturer, be the number of sleeping positions in the motor home.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a folding or removable seat is positioned at one
  or more wheelchair locations, the greater of the following shall be
  used for the purposes of subsection (1):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the number of designated seating positions that the
    seat contains, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of wheelchair locations.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2009-318, s. 2
- SOR/2011-264, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-2.3-20091209.html "Link to previous version of section 2.3"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Number of Designated Seating Positions]{.HTitleText1} {#h-478556 .Part}

- **[[2.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  subsection (3), [[measurement zone]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
  zone extending from a transverse vertical plane that is 150 mm behind
  the front leading surface of a seating surface to a transverse
  vertical plane that is 250 mm behind that front leading surface,
  measured horizontally and longitudinally.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a location in a vehicle that is likely to be used
  as a seating position has a seating surface width of at least 700 mm,
  the number of designated seating positions at that location shall be
  determined by using whichever of the following formulae is applicable
  and rounding the quotient down to the nearest whole number:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the location has a seating surface width of less
    than 1 400 mm,

    ::: {#478561 .Paragraph}
    N = W / X

    where

    [N]{.dfn}
    :   is the number of designated seating positions,

    [W]{.dfn}
    :   is the seating surface width in millimetres, and

    [X]{.dfn}
    :   is 350 or, at the option of the manufacturer, a number that is
        chosen by the manufacturer and is less than 350 but not less
        than 330; and
    :::

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the location has a seating surface width of
    1 400 mm or more,

    ::: {#478567 .Paragraph}
    N = W / X

    where

    [N]{.dfn}
    :   is the number of designated seating positions,

    [W]{.dfn}
    :   is the seating surface width in millimetres, and

    [X]{.dfn}
    :   is 450 or, at the option of the manufacturer, a number that is
        chosen by the manufacturer and is less than 450 but not less
        than 330.
    :::

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Adjacent seating surfaces are considered to form a
  single seating surface, unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the seating surfaces are separated by a fixed
    trimmed surface that has an unpadded top surface and that has a
    width of not less than 140 mm in each transverse vertical plane, as
    measured in the measurement zone;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the seating surfaces are separated by a void whose
    cross section in each transverse vertical plane within the
    measurement zone is a rectangle that is not less than 140 mm wide
    and not less than 140 mm deep, and the top edge of the cross section
    in each of those planes is congruent with the transverse horizontal
    line that intersects the lowest point on the portion of the top
    profile of the seating surfaces that lie within the plane;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} interior trim interrupts a line drawn between the
    H-points of adjacent seating surfaces; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the seating surfaces are adjacent outboard seats,
    and the lateral distance between each point on the seat cushion of
    one seat and each point on the seat cushion of the other seat is not
    less than 140 mm.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Folding, removable and adjustable seats must be
  measured in the configuration that results in the largest seating
  surface width.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The number of designated seating positions in a bench
  type seat in a school bus shall be the number of seating positions
  determined in accordance with Technical Standards Document No. 222,
  School Bus Passenger Seating and Crash Protection.

#### [Transitional Provision]{.HTitleText2} {#transitional-provision .SchedHeadL2}

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} Until September 1, 2020, the number of designated
  seating positions referred to in subsection (5) may conform to the
  requirements of this section as it read on the day before the day on
  which this subsection came into force.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2009-318, s. 2
- SOR/2011-264, s. 2
- SOR/2018-143-2, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-2.4-20120901.html "Link to previous version of section 2.4"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Prescribed Class of a Vehicle]{.HTitleText1} {#h-478583 .Part}

- **[[2.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purpose of determining the prescribed class of a vehicle, any
  wheelchair location is considered to be equivalent to four locations
  for the purpose of determining the designated seating capacity if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle was designed to have a designated
    seating capacity of more than 10; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any of the intended designated seating positions
    are replaced by a wheelchair location.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of determining the prescribed class
  of a vehicle resulting from the alteration of a bus by the replacement
  of any designated seating position with a wheelchair location, the
  location may, at the option of the manufacturer, be considered to be
  equivalent to four locations for the purpose of determining the
  designated seating capacity.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2011-264, s. 2
:::

## [National Safety Marks]{.HTitleText1} {#h-478591 .Part}

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of these Regulations, the symbol set out in Schedule I is
  prescribed as the national safety mark.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 3(2) of the Act, on
  application by a company, the Minister may, in the form set out in
  Schedule II, authorize the company to apply the national safety mark
  to a vehicle.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-491, s. 1
- SOR/82-482, s. 2
- SOR/95-147, s. 2
- SOR/2017-57, s. 1

  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-3-20170503.html "Link to previous version of section 3"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Prescribed Classes of Vehicles]{.HTitleText1} {#h-478595 .Part}

- **[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of sections 4 and 5 of the Act, the classes of vehicles set
  out in Schedule III and incomplete vehicles are prescribed classes of
  vehicles.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The prescribed classes of vehicles do not include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} vehicles whose date of manufacture is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a bus, earlier than January 1,
      1971, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in all other cases, 15 years or more earlier
      than the date of its importation; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} restricted-use vehicles that have motive power and
    are designed so that their speed attainable in 1.6 km (1 mile) is
    less than 32 km/h.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/82-482, s. 3
- SOR/88-268, s. 2
- SOR/95-147, s. 2
- SOR/2002-55, s. 2

  role="button"}

## [Safety and Emission Requirements]{.HTitleText1} {#h-478602 .Part}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/97-376, s. 1

\]
:::

- **[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Each
  requirement set out in Schedules IV to VI is prescribed as a Canada
  Motor Vehicle Safety Standard for vehicles of prescribed classes.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every vehicle, other than an incomplete vehicle, must
  conform to each standard that is referred to by number in column I of
  Schedule III and opposite which the letter "X" is set out in the
  subcolumn designating the class or subclass of the vehicle.

- [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2003-2, s. 46\]]{.Repealed}

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every incomplete vehicle shall conform to each
  standard set out in Schedules IV, V.1 and VI for completed vehicles to
  the extent that the standard governs the components that are fitted on
  the incomplete vehicle.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/95-147, s. 2
- SOR/97-376, s. 2
- SOR/2002-55, s. 3
- SOR/2003-2, s. 46
- SOR/2003-272, s. 3

## [Interprovincial Shipments]{.HTitleText1} {#h-478614 .Part}

- **[[5.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding section 4 of the
  Act, a manufacturer may ship from one province to another, or deliver
  to any person for the purpose of so shipping, a vehicle of a
  prescribed class manufactured in Canada that does not bear the
  national safety mark if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the manufacturer signs and files with the Minister
    a declaration setting out the information referred to in subsection
    (2);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle is being shipped or delivered for the
    purpose of exhibition, demonstration, evaluation or testing; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle is destroyed or returned to the
    province of origin within one year.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A declaration made pursuant to subsection (1) shall
  set out the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the manufacturer of the
    vehicle;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the month and year of the date of manufacture of
    the vehicle;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the prescribed class, make, model and vehicle
    identification number of the vehicle;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the use to be made of the vehicle;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the estimated period of time the vehicle will be
    used on public roads;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} whether the vehicle will be destroyed or returned
    to the province of origin after completion of the purpose for which
    it was shipped or delivered; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the date the vehicle will be destroyed or returned
    to the province of origin.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The declarations made in accordance with
  subsection (2)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} shall be filed prior to shipping or delivering the
    vehicle; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a company whose world production of
    vehicles is 2,500 or more a year, may be filed with the Minister
    quarterly.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/95-494, s. 1
- [SOR/2020-22, s. 7](#1225745-1226382){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1225745-1226382.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2020-22, s. 16](#1225775-1226583){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1225775-1226583.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-5.1-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 5.1"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Gross Vehicle Weight Rating]{.HTitleText1} {#h-478633 .Part}

**[[5.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5.2 .sectionLabel}** The gross vehicle
weight rating of a vehicle shall be not less than the sum of

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the unloaded vehicle mass,

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the cargo-carrying capacity,

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the product obtained by multiplying the designated
  seating capacity by 54 kg, in the case of a school bus, or by 68 kg,
  in any other case, and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle having living or sanitary
  accommodations, the mass of its fresh water, hot water and propane
  tanks, but not its waste water tanks, when full.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/98-125, s. 2
- SOR/2008-258, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-5.2-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 5.2"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Maximum Speed]{.HTitleText1} {#h-478641 .Part}

- **[[5.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5.3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  maximum speed of a motorcycle shall be measured in accordance with
  International Organization for Standardization standard ISO 7117:1995,
  Motorcycles --- Measurement of Maximum Speed.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Wherever the term [[motorcycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  is used in International Organization for Standardization standard ISO
  7117:1995, Motorcycles --- Measurement of Maximum Speed, it shall have
  the same meaning as [[motorcycle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in
  subsection 2(1) of these Regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2011-263, s. 2
:::

## [Compliance Label]{.HTitleText1} {#h-478647 .Part}

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to subsections (1.1) and 6.6(1), a company that manufactures a vehicle
  of a prescribed class that is a completed vehicle and that meets the
  requirements of these Regulations must ensure that the vehicle bears a
  compliance label displaying at least

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the manufacturer of the completed
    vehicle;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the month and year of the date of manufacture of
    the vehicle;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a drawing at least 13 mm in diameter depicting the
    national safety mark, as set out in Schedule I, and showing in its
    centre, in figures at least 2 mm in height, the authorization number
    assigned by the Minister to the company pursuant to section 3;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle identification number;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a passenger car, multi-purpose
    passenger vehicle, low-speed vehicle, three-wheeled vehicle, truck,
    bus, trailer, trailer converter dolly or motorcycle,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the gross vehicle weight rating, expressed in
      kilograms, clearly identified by the words "Gross Vehicle Weight
      Rating" and "[Poids nominal brut du véhicule]{.otherLang
      lang="fr"}" or the abbreviations "GVWR" and "PNBV", and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the gross axle weight rating, expressed in
      kilograms, for each axle of the vehicle listed in order from front
      to rear and clearly identified by the words "Gross Axle Weight
      Ratings" and "[Poids nominal brut sur l'essieu]{.otherLang
      lang="fr"}" or the abbreviations "GAWR" and "PNBE", unless the
      information is set out on the vehicle placard or on the tire
      inflation pressure label referred to in section S4.3 of Technical
      Standards Document No. 110, Tire Selection and Rims for Motor
      Vehicles With a GVWR of 4,536 kg or Less, or on the tire
      information label referred to in section S5.3(b) of Technical
      Standards Document No. 120, Tire Selection and Rims for Motor
      Vehicles With a GVWR of More Than 4,536 kg;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the type of vehicle, in both official languages, or
    the word "TYPE" along with the characters set out below that
    correspond to the type of vehicle:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} "AMB" to refer to an ambulance,

    - [(i.1)]{.lawlabel} "AT/PA" to refer to an auto transporter,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} "ATV/VTT" to refer to an all-terrain vehicle,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} "B/A" to refer to a bus,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} "BT/RA" to refer to a bus trailer,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} "CD/CCC" to refer to a C-dolly,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} "EMC/MCH" to refer to an enclosed motorcycle,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} "HHT/RL" to refer to a heavy hauler trailer,

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} "LSM/MVL" to refer to a limited-speed
      motorcycle,

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} "LDD/CRC" to refer to a load divider dolly,

    - [(ix.1)]{.lawlabel} "LSV/VBV" to refer to a low-speed vehicle,

    - [(x)]{.lawlabel} "MH/AC" to refer to a motor home,

    - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} "MC" to refer to an open motorcycle,

    - [(xii)]{.lawlabel} "MPV/VTUM" to refer to a multi-purpose
      passenger vehicle,

    - [(xiii)]{.lawlabel} "PC/VT" to refer to a passenger car,

    - [(xiv)]{.lawlabel} "RUM/MUR" to refer to a restricted-use vehicle,

    - [(xv)]{.lawlabel} "SB/AS" to refer to a school bus,

    - [(xvi)]{.lawlabel} "TRA/REM" to refer to a trailer,

    - [(xvii)]{.lawlabel} "TCD/CDC" to refer to a trailer converter
      dolly,

    - [(xviii)]{.lawlabel} "TRI" to refer to a motor tricycle,

    - [(xix)]{.lawlabel} "TRU/CAM" to refer to a truck,

    - [(xx)]{.lawlabel} "TT/CT" to refer to a truck tractor,

    - [(xxi)]{.lawlabel} "TWV/VTR" to refer to a three-wheeled vehicle,

    - [(xxii)]{.lawlabel} "SNO/MNG" to refer to a snowmobile, and

    - [(xxiii)]{.lawlabel} "MFSAB/AMAS" to refer to a multifunction
      school activity bus;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a C-dolly, the mounting height,
    expressed in both official languages, of the coupling when the
    C-dolly is not loaded; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a trailer that is designed to tow a
    C-dolly, the mounting height, expressed in both official languages,
    of the coupling when the trailer is not loaded;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2002-55, s. 4\]]{.Repealed}

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} If an incomplete vehicle manufacturer or an
  intermediate manufacturer assumes legal responsibility for the
  completed vehicle's conformity to the requirements of these
  Regulations, the incomplete vehicle manufacturer or the intermediate
  manufacturer, as the case may be, shall ensure that a compliance label
  is applied to the completed vehicle in accordance with this section,
  except that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the incomplete vehicle manufacturer or
    the intermediate manufacturer, as the case may be, shall appear on
    the compliance label instead of the name of the manufacturer
    referred to in paragraph (1)(a); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date of manufacture of the completed vehicle
    may be no earlier than the date on which manufacturing operations on
    the vehicle are completed by the incomplete vehicle manufacturer and
    no later than the date on which manufacturing operations on the
    vehicle are completed by the final-stage manufacturer.

- [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} If an incomplete vehicle manufacturer assumes legal
  responsibility for the completed vehicle's conformity to the
  requirements of these Regulations, the provisions related to vehicles
  manufactured in stages set out in sections 6.1 to 6.6 do not apply.

- [(1.3)]{.lawlabel} If an intermediate manufacturer assumes legal
  responsibility for the completed vehicle's conformity to the
  requirements of these Regulations, the provisions related to vehicles
  manufactured in stages set out in sections 6.3 to 6.6 do not apply.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The drawing referred to in paragraph (1)(c)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} may be displayed on a label applied to the vehicle
    beside the compliance label; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an imported vehicle, may be replaced
    by the following statement indicating that the vehicle conforms to
    the standards --- prescribed under these Regulations --- that are
    applicable on the date of manufacture:

    > ::: QuotedText
    > "THIS VEHICLE CONFORMS TO ALL APPLICABLE STANDARDS PRESCRIBED
    > UNDER THE CANADIAN MOTOR VEHICLE SAFETY REGULATIONS IN EFFECT ON
    > THE DATE OF MANUFACTURE / [CE VÉHICULE EST CONFORME À TOUTES LES
    > NORMES QUI LUI SONT APPLICABLES EN VERTU DU RÈGLEMENT SUR LA
    > SÉCURITÉ DES VÉHICULES AUTOMOBILES DU CANADA EN VIGUEUR À LA DATE
    > DE SA FABRICATION]{.otherLang lang="fr"}".
    > :::

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The compliance label shall be applied

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a bus, multi-purpose passenger
    vehicle, three-wheeled vehicle, passenger car, truck or low-speed
    vehicle,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to the hinge pillar, door latch post or the door
      edge that meets the door latch post next to the driver's seating
      position,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to the left side of the instrument panel or the
      inward-facing surface of the door next to the driver's seating
      position, where it is impracticable to apply the label in
      accordance with subparagraph (i), or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} to a conspicuous and readily accessible
      location, where it is impracticable to apply the label in
      accordance with subparagraph (i) or (ii);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a trailer, to the forward half of
    the left side of the trailer so that it is easily readable from
    outside the trailer without moving any part of the trailer;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a motorcycle, to a permanent part of
    the motorcycle as close as is practicable to the intersection of the
    head tube and the handlebars, so that it is easily readable without
    moving any part of the motorcycle except the steering system;

  - [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a restricted-use vehicle, to a
    permanent part of the vehicle as close as is practicable to the
    intersection of the head tube and the handlebars or of the steering
    column and the steering wheel, so that it is easily readable without
    moving any part of the vehicle except the steering system; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a snowmobile, to the rear half of
    the right side of the vehicle so that it is easily readable from
    outside the vehicle without moving any part of the vehicle.

- [(4) to (7)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2002-55, s. 4\]]{.Repealed}

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a multi-purpose passenger vehicle or
  bus manufactured from a cutaway chassis, a motor home or a
  recreational trailer, the compliance label shall display the following
  information in both official languages:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cargo-carrying capacity of the vehicle;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the designated seating capacity, except in the case
    of a recreational trailer;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a motor home, the total mass of the
    occupants, which is obtained by multiplying the designated seating
    capacity by 68 kg; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a motor home or a recreational
    trailer,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the mass of the fresh water, hot water and waste
      water tanks when full, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the displayed cargo-carrying
      capacity is determined with the fresh water and hot water tanks
      full and the waste water tanks empty.

- [(8.1)]{.lawlabel} The information referred to in subsection (8) may
  be displayed on a separate label applied to the vehicle beside the
  compliance label or in a conspicuous or readily accessible location.

- [(9)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a limited-speed motorcycle and a
  low-speed vehicle, a statement in both official languages that the use
  of the vehicle may be restricted by provincial authorities to certain
  roads shall appear on the compliance label or on a separate label
  permanently applied to the vehicle in a conspicuous location.

- [(10)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a restricted-use vehicle, a statement
  in both official languages that the vehicle is a restricted-use
  vehicle or all-terrain vehicle and is not intended for use on public
  roads must appear on the compliance label or on a separate label
  permanently applied to the vehicle in a conspicuous location.

- [(11)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a model of vehicle in respect of
  which the Minister has made an exemption order under section 9 of the
  Act, the compliance label or information label, as the case may be,
  must also display the words "Exemption/[Dispense]{.otherLang
  lang="fr"} \[indicate here the identifier set out in the exemption
  order\]".

- [(12) and (13)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2000-182, s.
  2\]]{.Repealed}

## [Vehicles Manufactured in Stages]{.HTitleText1} {#h-478719 .Part}

### [Incomplete Vehicle Manufacturer's Document]{.HTitleText2} {#h-478720 .Subheading}

- **[[6.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  incomplete vehicle manufacturer shall, at or before the time of
  delivery of an incomplete vehicle, provide to the intermediate
  manufacturer, the final-stage manufacturer or other purchaser, as the
  case may be, an incomplete vehicle document that contains the
  following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and mailing address of the incomplete
    vehicle manufacturer;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the month and year the incomplete vehicle
    manufacturer performed its last manufacturing operation on the
    incomplete vehicle;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle identification number;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the GVWR, expressed in kilograms, intended for the
    vehicle when it is a completed vehicle;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the GAWR, expressed in kilograms, intended for each
    axle of the vehicle when it is a completed vehicle, listed in order
    from front to rear, except that the GAWR for consecutive axles that
    have identical GAWRs when equipped with tires that have the same
    designated tire size may be stated once followed by the words "each"
    and "[chacun]{.otherLang lang="fr"}";

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a list of the types of vehicles referred to in
    paragraph 6(1)(f) into which the incomplete vehicle is designed to
    be manufactured; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the numbers of the prescribed standards that apply,
    at the date specified in paragraph (b), in respect of each type of
    vehicle listed, followed in each case by one or more of the
    following statements, as applicable:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the completed vehicle will
      conform to the standard if no alterations are made to the
      components of the incomplete vehicle that are identified by the
      incomplete vehicle manufacturer (for example, CMVSS 104 --- This
      vehicle, when completed, will conform to Standard 104, Windshield
      Wiping and Washing System, if no alterations are made to the
      windshield or the windshield wiping and washing system),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the completed vehicle will
      conform to the standard if the vehicle is manufactured in
      accordance with the conditions specified by the incomplete vehicle
      manufacturer (for example, CMVSS 121 --- This vehicle, when
      completed, will conform to Standard 121, Air Brake Systems, if it
      does not exceed any of the GAWRs, if the centre of gravity at GVWR
      is not higher than 2.75 m above the ground and if no alterations
      are made in any brake system component), and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a statement that conformity to the standard
      cannot be determined based upon the components that are fitted on
      the incomplete vehicle and that the incomplete vehicle
      manufacturer makes no representation as to conformity with the
      standard.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The document shall be kept in a weather-resistant
  container that is attached to the vehicle in a conspicuous and readily
  accessible location, or it may be sent directly to an intermediate
  manufacturer, a final-stage manufacturer or other purchaser, as the
  case may be.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2002-55, s. 5
:::

### [Incomplete Vehicle Manufacturer's Information Label]{.HTitleText2} {#h-478736 .Subheading}

- **[[6.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every incomplete vehicle
  manufacturer shall apply to every incomplete vehicle it manufactures
  an information label that displays the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a statement, in both official languages, that the
    vehicle is an incomplete vehicle;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the incomplete vehicle manufacturer;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the month and year the incomplete vehicle
    manufacturer performed its last manufacturing operation on the
    incomplete vehicle;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle identification number;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the GVWR intended for the vehicle when it is a
    completed vehicle, expressed in kilograms, clearly identified by the
    words "Gross Vehicle Weight Rating" and "[Poids nominal brut du
    véhicule]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" or the abbreviations "GVWR" and
    "PNBV";

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the GAWR, expressed in kilograms, intended for each
    axle of the vehicle when it is a completed vehicle, listed in order
    from front to rear and clearly identified by the words "Gross Axle
    Weight Ratings" and "[Poids nominal brut sur l'essieu]{.otherLang
    lang="fr"}" or the abbreviations "GAWR" and "PNBE", except that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the GAWR for consecutive axles that have
      identical GAWRs when equipped with tires that have the same
      designated tire size may be stated once followed by the words
      "each" and "[chacun]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the information need not appear on the label if
      it is set out on the vehicle placard or the tire inflation
      pressure label referred to in section S4.3 of Technical Standards
      Document No. 110, Tire Selection and Rims for Motor Vehicles With
      a GVWR of 4,536 kg or Less, or on the tire information label
      referred to in section S5.3(b) of Technical Standards Document
      No. 120, Tire Selection and Rims for Motor Vehicles With a GVWR of
      More Than 4,536 kg; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle manufactured in Canada for
    sale in Canada, a drawing of at least 20 mm in diameter depicting
    the national safety mark set out in Schedule I and showing in its
    centre, in figures of at least 2 mm in height, the authorization
    number assigned by the Minister to the company under section 3.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), the information label
  shall be applied

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to the hinge pillar, door latch post or the door
    edge that meets the door latch post next to the driver's seating
    position;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to the left side of the instrument panel or the
    inward-facing surface of the door next to the driver's seating
    position, if it is impracticable to apply the label in accordance
    with paragraph (a); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to a conspicuous and readily accessible location,
    if it is impracticable to apply the label in accordance with
    paragraph (a) or (b) or if the vehicle does not have the components
    described in paragraph (a) or (b).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a stripped or cowl chassis, the
  information label may be applied to a conspicuous and readily
  accessible location on the steering column.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The drawing referred to in paragraph (1)(g) may be
  displayed on a label applied to the vehicle beside the incomplete
  vehicle manufacturer's information label.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2002-55, s. 5
- SOR/2005-342, s. 2
- SOR/2008-258, s. 4
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-6.2-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 6.2"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Intermediate Manufacturer's Document]{.HTitleText2} {#h-478756 .Subheading}

- **[[6.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every intermediate manufacturer of
  an incomplete vehicle shall, at or before the time of delivery of the
  incomplete vehicle to the subsequent manufacturer, provide to the
  subsequent manufacturer, in the manner specified in subsection 6.1(2),
  the incomplete vehicle document that was provided by the previous
  manufacturer.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An intermediate manufacturer shall, before complying
  with subsection (1), make an addendum to the incomplete vehicle
  document that contains the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} its name and mailing address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a clear and precise description of all the changes
    it has made to the incomplete vehicle; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if any of the changes affect the validity of a
    statement made by the incomplete vehicle manufacturer in accordance
    with paragraph 6.1(1)(g), an indication of the amendments that must
    be made to those statements to reflect the changes made by the
    intermediate manufacturer.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2002-55, s. 5
:::

### [Intermediate Manufacturer's Information Label]{.HTitleText2} {#h-478765 .Subheading}

- **[[6.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), an
  intermediate manufacturer shall apply to every incomplete vehicle,
  beside the information label of the previous manufacturer, an
  information label that displays the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a statement, in both official languages, that the
    vehicle is an incomplete vehicle;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the intermediate manufacturer;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a statement, in both official languages, that the
    company is an intermediate manufacturer;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the month and year in which the intermediate
    manufacturer performed its last manufacturing operation on the
    incomplete vehicle; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle manufactured in Canada for
    sale in Canada, a drawing of at least 20 mm in diameter depicting
    the national safety mark set out in Schedule I and showing in its
    centre, in figures of at least 2 mm in height, the authorization
    number assigned by the Minister to the company under section 3.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The drawing referred to in paragraph (1)(e) may be
  displayed on a label applied to the vehicle beside the intermediate
  manufacturer's information label.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the information label applied to an incomplete
  vehicle by the previous manufacturer is not in a location described in
  paragraph 6.2(2)(a) or (b),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information label shall be applied in a
    location specified in paragraph 6.2(2)(a) or (b), or in a
    conspicuous and readily accessible location if it is impracticable
    to conform to paragraph 6.2(2)(a) or (b); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (4), the information label
    shall display the GVWR and GAWRs set out on the label applied by the
    previous manufacturer.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} If an intermediate manufacturer increases the GVWR or
  the GAWRs above those referred to in paragraphs 6.1(1)(d) and (e), the
  intermediate manufacturer shall ensure that the new ratings are
  displayed on that intermediate manufacturer's information label and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} are increased in accordance with the written
    recommendations of the incomplete vehicle manufacturer or, if
    applicable, of another previous manufacturer; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} are within the load-carrying capacity of the
    vehicle's components when the vehicle is loaded for its intended use
    as a completed vehicle.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2002-55, s. 5
:::

### [Final-stage Manufacturer's Document]{.HTitleText2} {#h-478782 .Subheading}

- **[[6.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6.5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  final-stage manufacturer shall make an addendum to the incomplete
  vehicle document that contains the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} its name and mailing address; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a clear and precise description of all the changes
    that it has made to the incomplete vehicle.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The final-stage manufacturer shall retain and make
  available to the Minister, on request, the incomplete vehicle
  documentation referred to in subsection (1) and sections 6.1 and 6.3,
  for a period of no less than five years after the date manufacturing
  operations on the vehicle are completed by the final-stage
  manufacturer.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2002-55, s. 5
:::

### [Final-stage Manufacturer's Compliance Label]{.HTitleText2} {#h-478790 .Subheading}

- **[[6.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every final-stage manufacturer shall
  choose a date of manufacture for a completed vehicle that may be no
  earlier than the date specified by the incomplete vehicle manufacturer
  on its information label but no later than the date manufacturing
  operations on the vehicle are completed by the final-stage
  manufacturer and shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} complete the incomplete vehicle in such a manner
    that the completed vehicle conforms to the standards prescribed for
    a completed vehicle of that prescribed class as of the date chosen
    by the final-stage manufacturer; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} apply to the completed vehicle a compliance label
    in accordance with section 6, except that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date of manufacture referred to in
      paragraph 6(1)(b) is the date of manufacture chosen by the
      final-stage manufacturer, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (2), the GVWR and GAWRs
      shall be those set out on the label applied by the previous
      manufacturer.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a final-stage manufacturer increases the GVWR or
  the GAWRs above those referred to in paragraphs 6.1(1)(d) and (e) and
  subsection 6.4(4) or new ratings have been displayed on an
  intermediate manufacturer's information label, the final-stage
  manufacturer shall ensure that the new ratings are displayed on the
  compliance label for the completed vehicle and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} are increased in accordance with the written
    recommendations of the incomplete vehicle manufacturer or, if
    applicable, of another previous manufacturer; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} are within the load-carrying capacity of the
    vehicle's components when the vehicle is loaded for its intended use
    as a completed vehicle.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2002-55, s. 5
- [SOR/2020-22, s. 16](#1225775-1226585){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1225775-1226585.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-6.6-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 6.6"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** All the labels applied to a
vehicle under sections 6, 6.2, 6.4 and 6.6 shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} be permanently attached to the vehicle;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} be resistant to or protected against any weather
  condition to which the label may be exposed;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} have lettering that is

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} clear and indelible,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} indented, embossed or in a colour that contrasts
    with the background colour of the label, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in block capitals and numerals not less than 2 mm
    in height; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} have metric units identified by the appropriate name
  or symbol.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/82-482, s. 5
- SOR/95-147, s. 2
- SOR/2002-55, s. 6
:::

**[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/2002-55,
s. 7\]]{.Repealed}

## [Altered Vehicle]{.HTitleText1} {#h-478813 .Part}

- **[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  company alters a vehicle, other than an incomplete vehicle or a truck
  tractor not fitted with a fifth wheel coupling, that was in conformity
  with these Regulations in such a manner that its stated GVWR and GAWR
  are no longer accurate, or if the company alters the vehicle otherwise
  than by the addition, substitution or removal of readily attachable
  components such as mirrors or tire and rim assemblies or by minor
  finishing operations, the company shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the compliance label and information
    label, if applicable, remain on the vehicle;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} respect the gross axle weight ratings and gross
    vehicle weight rating of the vehicle recommended by the original
    manufacturer or, where the company increases the ratings, ensure
    that they are

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} increased in accordance with the original
      manufacturer's written recommendations, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} within the load-carrying capacity of the
      vehicle's components when the altered vehicle is loaded for its
      intended use;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the vehicle conforms to the standards
    referred to in subsection 5(2), in respect of the work carried out
    by the company to alter the vehicle; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (2), apply to the vehicle an
    additional label displaying

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the words "THIS VEHICLE WAS ALTERED BY / [CE
      VÉHICLE A ÉTÉ MODIFIÉ PAR]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" or "ALTERED BY /
      [MODIFIÉ PAR]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" followed by the name of the
      company that altered the vehicle,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the month and year during which the alteration
      of the vehicle was completed,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the drawing referred to in paragraph 6(1)(c),

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with paragraph 6(1)(e), the new
      gross vehicle weight rating and gross axle weight ratings of the
      vehicle as altered, where they differ from those shown on the
      original compliance label,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with paragraph 6(1)(f), the type of
      vehicle, where it differs from the type shown on the original
      compliance label, and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a multi-purpose passenger vehicle
      or bus manufactured from a cutaway chassis, a motor home or a
      recreational trailer, the information referred to in subsection
      6(8).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The drawing referred to in paragraph (1)(c) may be
  displayed on a label applied to the vehicle beside the compliance
  label.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a motor home or a recreational
  trailer, the information referred to in subparagraph (1)(c)(vi) may be
  displayed on a separate label applied to the vehicle beside the
  compliance label or in a conspicuous or readily accessible location.

::: HistoricalNote

:::

## [Records]{.HTitleText1} {#h-478832 .Part}

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company shall maintain --- for each vehicle to which it applies the
  national safety mark or that it imports into Canada --- the records
  referred to in paragraph 5(1)(g) of the Act and retain those records,
  in paper form or in readily readable electronic form, for a period of
  at least five years after the day on which the vehicle is manufactured
  or imported.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the records referred to in subsection (1) are
  maintained by a person on behalf of a company, the company shall keep
  the name and address of that person.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} On request in writing from an inspector, a company
  shall send to that inspector a copy, in either official language, of
  the records referred to in subsection (1) within

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 30 working days after the mailing of the request;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where the records must be translated, 45 working
    days after the mailing of the request.

::: HistoricalNote

## [Importation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-478841 .Part}

### [Vehicles Imported Under Sections 5 and 6 of the Act]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1226390 .Subheading}

- **[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of paragraph 5(1)(b) of the Act, a person that imports a
  vehicle of a prescribed class must, before importation, produce
  evidence that the vehicle conforms to the standards set out in these
  Regulations by providing the Minister with the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the importer's name, mailing address, telephone
    number, facsimile number and email address and, if the importer is a
    company, the contact information of a contact person at the company;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the manufacturer of the vehicle;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the vehicle is to be presented
    for importation;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the prescribed class, make, model, model year and
    vehicle identification number of the vehicle;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the status of the vehicle;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the month and year of the date of manufacture of
    the vehicle; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a statement indicating that the vehicle bears an
    information label or compliance label, as the case may be, or, if
    the importer is an individual, a statement from the manufacturer
    that the vehicle conformed to the standards --- prescribed under
    these Regulations --- that were applicable on the date of
    manufacture.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), a person that imports at
  least 2,500 vehicles annually is not required to provide the
  information referred to in subsection (1) unless the Minister makes a
  written request and, in that case, the person must provide that
  information to the Minister within 30 days after the day on which the
  request is made.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 5(3) of the Act does not apply to a
  vehicle sold at the retail level in the United States or a prescribed
  vehicle from Mexico, if the vehicle does not meet the condition set
  out in paragraph 7(2)(a) of the Act.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A company that imports a vehicle under subsection
  5(3) of the Act must provide the Minister, before importation, with
  the information referred to in paragraphs (1)(a) to (d) and the
  following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a list of the applicable requirements of these
    Regulations to which the vehicle does not conform;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a statement from the manufacturer that completed
    the main assembly of the vehicle indicating that the vehicle, when
    completed in accordance with the manufacturer's instructions, will
    conform to the standards --- prescribed under these Regulations ---
    that were applicable on the date of manufacture;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a statement indicating that the vehicle will be
    completed in accordance with the manufacturer's instructions; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name of the company that will complete the
    vehicle.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-940, s. 4

### [Vehicles Imported Temporarily for Special Purposes]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1226408 .Subheading}

**[[11.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.1 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
paragraph 7(1)(a) of the Act, the prescribed purposes for which a
vehicle may be imported temporarily are the following:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} exhibition;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} demonstration;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} evaluation;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} testing;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} further manufacturing prior to export;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the conducting of works or operations that require a
  specially designed vehicle for entertainment industry productions,
  civil engineering projects or similar works or operations;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an armoured vehicle, use by a law
  enforcement agency; and

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle that is licensed in the
  United States, a visit to Canada by its owner, if the owner has a
  residential address in Canada and is the holder of a driver's licence
  issued in Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-22, s. 9](#1225763-1226419){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1225763-1226419.html"
  role="button"}
:::

**[[11.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.2 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
paragraph 7(1)(a) of the Act, a vehicle imported temporarily for special
purposes must have a vehicle identification number that conforms to the
requirements of section 115 of Schedule IV or, if there is no vehicle
identification number, a serial number.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-22, s. 9](#1225763-1226422){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1225763-1226422.html"
  role="button"}
:::

- **[[11.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The declaration referred to in
  paragraph 7(1)(a) of the Act must be filed with the Minister, be
  signed by the person importing the vehicle and contain the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle identification number or, if there is
    no vehicle identification number, the serial number;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the importer's name, mailing address, telephone
    number, facsimile number and email address and, if the importer is a
    company, the contact information of a contact person at the company;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the vehicle is to be presented
    for importation;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name of the manufacturer of the vehicle;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the month and year of the date of manufacture;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the type of vehicle;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the prescribed class, make, model and model year of
    the vehicle;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the purpose for which the vehicle is being imported
    and a statement that it will be used only for that purpose;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a statement indicating that the vehicle will remain
    in Canada for a period of not more than one year or the period
    specified by the Minister, as applicable;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a statement indicating that the vehicle will be
    exported or destroyed before the end of the one-year period or the
    period specified by the Minister, as applicable;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the vehicle conforms to the
    requirements of section 11.2; and

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} if the declaration is signed by a representative, a
    statement by the importer indicating that the representative is
    authorized to sign.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), a person that imports at
  least 2,500 vehicles annually is not required to include the
  information referred to in paragraphs (1)(a), (c), (e) to (g) and (k)
  in the declaration but must provide that information to the Minister
  within 30 days after the day on which a written request is made by the
  Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-22, s. 9](#1225763-1226439){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1225763-1226439.html"
  role="button"}
:::

**[[11.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.4 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subsection 7(1.02) of the Act, a person that imports a vehicle imported
temporarily for special purposes may donate the vehicle to a public
museum or to an educational institution designated by the Canada Revenue
Agency, under the terms of an agreement that describes how the recipient
will ensure that the vehicle will not be driven or drawn on public
roads.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-22, s. 9](#1225763-1226442){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1225763-1226442.html"
  role="button"}
:::

**[[11.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.5 .sectionLabel}** A person who imports
fewer than 2,500 vehicles annually must provide, as applicable, evidence
of the exportation, destruction or donation of any vehicle imported
temporarily for special purposes to the Minister within 30 days after
the day on which the period referred to paragraph 7(1)(a) of the Act
ends.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-22, s. 9](#1225763-1226445){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1225763-1226445.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Vehicles Imported Under Subsections 7(2) and (2.1) of the Act]{.HTitleText2} {#h-478868 .Subheading}

#### [General]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1226447 .Subheading}

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  person designated for the purposes of subsections 7(2) and (2.1) of
  the Act is the entity with which the Department of Transport has
  entered into a contract to operate the national program for the
  registration, inspection and certification of imported vehicles, known
  as the registrar of imported vehicles.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsections 7(2) and (2.1) of the
  Act, a passenger car, multi-purpose passenger vehicle, truck or bus
  that is a "used vehicle" as defined in Article 2.1 of Chapter 2 of
  CUSMA is a prescribed vehicle from Mexico.

#### [Vehicles Sold at the Retail Level in the United States and Prescribed Vehicles from Mexico]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1226452 .Subheading}

- **[[12.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph
  7(2)(a) of the Act, the prescribed requirements that a vehicle sold at
  the retail level in the United States or a prescribed vehicle from
  Mexico must meet are the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle sold at the retail level
    in the United States --- other than a restricted-use vehicle or a
    snowmobile --- or a prescribed vehicle from Mexico, the vehicle
    conformed to the federal laws of the United States that were
    applicable on the date of manufacture, as indicated

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} by the vehicle's American compliance label, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in a statement from the manufacturer of the
      vehicle or, if there is more than one manufacturer, from each
      manufacturer indicating that the vehicle conformed to the
      requirements of Parts 541, 565, 571 and 581, chapter V, Title 49
      of the Code of Federal Regulations of the United States that were
      applicable on the date of manufacture;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a snowmobile sold at the retail
    level in the United States, it conformed to the standards published
    by the Snowmobile Safety and Certification Committee, Inc. that were
    applicable on the date of manufacture, as indicated

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} on the Snowmobile Safety and Certification
      Committee, Inc. certification label,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in a statement from the manufacturer indicating
      that the snowmobile was certified by the Snowmobile Safety and
      Certification Committee, Inc., or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in a statement from the manufacturer indicating
      that the snowmobile conformed to the standards referred to in
      section 1201 of Schedule VI on the date of manufacture; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a restricted-use vehicle designed to
    travel on two or four wheels and sold at the retail level in the
    United States, it conformed to the requirements of sections 108 and
    115 of Schedule IV on the date of manufacture.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph 7(2)(b) of the Act, the
  prescribed requirements that a vehicle sold at the retail level in the
  United States or a prescribed vehicle from Mexico must meet to be
  certified by the registrar of imported vehicles are the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any defect or non-compliance with respect to the
    vehicle's design, construction or functioning that was the subject
    of a notice of defect or non-compliance was corrected;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle conforms to the requirements of
    subsections 101(4) and (6) and sections 102 and 108 of Schedule IV;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle conforms to the requirements of
    subsection 114(4) of Schedule IV or was fitted with an electronic
    immobilization system on the date of manufacture or is fitted with
    an immobilization system that conforms to the National Standard of
    Canada CAN/ULC-S338-98, entitled Automobile Theft Deterrent
    Equipment and Systems: Electronic Immobilization (May, 1998),
    published by the Underwriters' Laboratories of Canada;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle equipped with a fuel
    system that uses LPG as a source of energy for its propulsion, it
    conforms to the requirements of section 301.1 of Schedule IV;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle equipped with a fuel
    system that uses CNG as a source of energy for its propulsion, it
    conforms to the requirements of section 301.2 of Schedule IV;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a school bus, it conforms to the
    requirements of section 111 of Schedule IV and, if applicable,
    section 301.1 or 301.2 of that Schedule;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a truck or multi-purpose passenger
    vehicle, it conforms to the requirements of section 111 of Schedule
    IV;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a trailer with a GVWR greater than 4
    536 kg, it conforms to the requirements of section 905 of Schedule
    IV;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a low-speed vehicle, it conforms to
    the requirements of section 500 of Schedule IV;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a C-dolly, it conforms to the
    requirements of section 903 of Schedule IV; and

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an enclosed motorcycle, motor
    tricycle, limited-speed motorcycle or three-wheeled vehicle, it
    conforms to the applicable requirements of Schedule IV.
