- **[[12.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The declaration referred to in
  paragraph 7(2)(b) of the Act, made by a person who imports a vehicle
  sold at the retail level in the United States or a prescribed vehicle
  from Mexico, must be signed by that person and contain the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle identification number;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the importer's name, mailing address, telephone
    number, facsimile number and email address and, if the importer is a
    company, the contact information of a contact person at the company;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the vehicle is to be presented
    for importation;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name of the manufacturer of the vehicle;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the type of vehicle;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the make, model and model year of the vehicle;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the status of the vehicle; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a statement indicating

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle whose status is salvage
      and repairable or an equivalent status, that, within one year
      after the day on which it is imported, the vehicle will be brought
      into conformity with the applicable requirements of subsection
      12.1(2), will be taken to the registrar of imported vehicles for
      inspection and will not be presented for registration under the
      laws of a province unless a Canadian certification label is issued
      in respect of the vehicle by the registrar of imported vehicles,
      or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, that, within 45 days after
      the day on which it is imported, the vehicle will be brought into
      conformity with the applicable requirements of subsection 12.1(2),
      will be taken to the registrar of imported vehicles for inspection
      and will not be presented for registration under the laws of a
      province unless a Canadian certification label is issued in
      respect of the vehicle by the registrar of imported vehicles.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a vehicle sold at the retail level in
  the United States, the declaration must also contain the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a statement indicating that the vehicle was sold at
    the retail level in the United States;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle other than a
    restricted-use vehicle or snowmobile, a statement indicating that
    the vehicle bears an American compliance label or a statement from
    the manufacturer indicating that the vehicle conformed to the
    requirements of Parts 541, 565, 571 and 581, chapter V, Title 49 of
    the Code of Federal Regulations of the United States that were
    applicable on the date of manufacture;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a snowmobile, a statement indicating
    that it bears a Snowmobile Safety and Certification Committee, Inc.
    certification label or a statement from the manufacturer indicating
    that the snowmobile was certified by the Snowmobile Safety and
    Certification Committee, Inc. or that the snowmobile conformed to
    the standards referred to in section 1201 of Schedule VI on the date
    of manufacture; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the month and year of the date of manufacture.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a prescribed vehicle from Mexico, the
  declaration must also contain the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the odometer reading;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the country in which the vehicle was
    last registered for use on public roads;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a statement indicating that the vehicle bears an
    American compliance label or a statement from the manufacturer
    indicating that the vehicle conformed to the requirements of Parts
    541, 565, 571 and 581, chapter V, Title 49 of the Code of Federal
    Regulations of the United States that were applicable on the date of
    manufacture; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the month and year of the date of manufacture.

:::

**[[12.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.3 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
paragraph 7(2)(b) of the Act, the prescribed period during which a
vehicle sold at the retail level in the United States or a prescribed
vehicle from Mexico must be brought into compliance with the
requirements of subsection 12.1(2) and certified by the registrar of
imported vehicles is the following:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle whose status is salvage and
  repairable or an equivalent status, one year beginning on the day on
  which the vehicle is imported; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, 45 days beginning on the day on
  which the vehicle is imported.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-22, s. 9](#1225763-1226505){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1225763-1226505.html"
  role="button"}
:::

- **[[12.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} After recording the vehicle in its
  registration system and inspecting the vehicle, the registrar of
  imported vehicles must certify the vehicle by issuing a Canadian
  certification label in both official languages if it determines that
  the vehicle conforms to the requirements of subsection 12.1(2).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Canadian certification label must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} display

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle identification number,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the necessary alterations have
      been made to bring the vehicle into conformity with the standards
      --- prescribed under these Regulations --- that were applicable on
      the date of manufacture,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the vehicle was altered, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a passenger car, truck,
      multi-purpose passenger vehicle, low-speed vehicle, bus, trailer,
      trailer converter dolly, motorcycle or three-wheeled vehicle that
      has been sold at the retail level in the United States and in the
      case of a prescribed vehicle from Mexico,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the gross vehicle weight rating of the vehicle,
        expressed in kilograms, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the gross axle weight rating for each axle of
        the vehicle, expressed in kilograms and listed in order from the
        front to the rear of the vehicle;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be permanently applied

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to the same surface as that to which an American
      compliance label is applied, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if there is no American compliance label on the
      vehicle, in the appropriate location referred to in subsection
      6(3);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} be resistant to or protected against any weather
    condition to which the label may be exposed; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} have lettering that is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} indelible,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} indented, embossed or in a colour that contrasts
      with the background colour of the label, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in block capitals and numerals not less than
      2 mm in height.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-22, s. 9](#1225763-1226525){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1225763-1226525.html"
  role="button"}
:::

#### [Vehicles Sold at the Retail Level in the United States and Prescribed Vehicles from Mexico --- Importation for Components]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1226526 .Subheading}

- **[[12.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.5
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The declaration referred to in
  subsection 7(2.1) of the Act, made by a person that imports for
  components a vehicle sold at the retail level in the United States or
  a prescribed vehicle from Mexico, must be signed by that person and
  contain the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle identification number;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the importer's name, mailing address, telephone
    number, facsimile number and email address and, if the importer is a
    company, the contact information of a contact person at the company;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the vehicle is to be presented
    for importation;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name of the manufacturer of the vehicle;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the type of vehicle;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the make, model and model year of the vehicle;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the status of the vehicle;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a statement indicating that the vehicle will not be
    presented for registration under the laws of a province;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a statement indicating that the declaration will be
    provided to the registrar of imported vehicles; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a statement indicating that the vehicle will be
    dismantled for its components.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a vehicle sold at the retail level in
  the United States, the declaration must also contain the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a statement indicating that the vehicle was sold at
    the retail level in the United States;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vehicle other than a
    restricted-use vehicle or snowmobile, a statement indicating that
    the vehicle bears an American compliance label or a statement from
    the manufacturer indicating that the vehicle conformed to the
    requirements of Parts 541, 565, 571 and 581, chapter V, Title 49 of
    the Code of Federal Regulations of the United States that were
    applicable on the date of manufacture;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a snowmobile, a statement indicating
    that it bears a Snowmobile Safety and Certification Committee, Inc.
    certification label or a statement from the manufacturer indicating
    that the snowmobile was certified by the Snowmobile Safety and
    Certification Committee, Inc. or that the snowmobile conformed to
    the standards referred to in section 1201 of Schedule VI on the date
    of manufacture; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the month and year of the date of manufacture.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a prescribed vehicle from Mexico, the
  declaration must also include the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the odometer reading;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the country in which the vehicle was
    last registered for use on public roads;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a statement indicating that the vehicle bears an
    American compliance label or a statement from the manufacturer
    indicating that the vehicle conformed to the requirements of Parts
    541, 565, 571 and 581, chapter V, Title 49 of the Code of Federal
    Regulations of the United States that were applicable on the date of
    manufacture; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the month and year of the date of manufacture.

**[[12.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.6 .sectionLabel}** After receiving the
declaration referred to in section 12.5, the registrar of imported
vehicles must record the vehicle in its registration system.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-22, s. 9](#1225763-1226553){.wb-lbx

## [Applications for Exemption]{.HTitleText1} {#h-478924 .Part}

- **[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  company applying for an exemption pursuant to section 9 of the Act
  must submit in writing to the Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} its name and address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the province or country under the laws of which it
    is established;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the number, title and text or substance of the
    standards from which exemption is sought and the duration of that
    exemption;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the reason for requesting an exemption;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the reasons for withholding from
    public disclosure any specific part of the information and data
    found in the application; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the reasons why the granting of the exemption would
    be in the public interest and consistent with the objectives of the
    Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2023-222, s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the basis of an application for exemption is the
  development of new safety features that are equivalent to or superior
  to those that conform to the prescribed standards, the applicant must
  include in the submission to the Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the new features;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the research, development and testing
    documentation establishing the innovative nature of the new
    features;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an analysis of how the level of performance of the
    new features is equivalent to or superior to the level of
    performance established by the prescribed standards, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a detailed description of how a vehicle equipped
      with the new features would, if exempted, differ from one that
      conforms to the prescribed standards, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the results of tests conducted on the new
      features that demonstrate a level of performance that is
      equivalent to or superior to that required by the prescribed
      standards;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} evidence that an exemption would facilitate the
    development or the field evaluation of the vehicle; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a statement as to whether the manufacturer intends,
    at the end of the exemption period,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to conform to the prescribed standards,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to apply for a further exemption, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} to request that the prescribed standards be
      amended to incorporate the new features.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the basis of an application for exemption is the
  development of new kinds of vehicles, technologies, vehicle systems or
  components, the applicant must include in the submission to the
  Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the research, development and testing
    documentation establishing that an exemption would not substantially
    diminish the safe performance of the vehicle, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a detailed description of how the vehicle
      equipped with the new kinds of vehicle systems or components
      would, if exempted, differ from one that conforms to the
      prescribed standards,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the reasons why not conforming to the prescribed
      standards does not substantially diminish the safe performance of
      the vehicle, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a discussion of other means of conformity that
      were considered and the reasons for rejecting each of them;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the reasons why an exemption would facilitate the
    development or the field evaluation of the vehicle; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a statement as to whether the company intends, at
    the end of the exemption period, the vehicle to conform to the
    prescribed standards.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister has made an exemption order under
  section 9 of the Act for a model of vehicle, the company must apply to
  every vehicle of that model a label that displays the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the features of the vehicle and the standards, by
    number and title, in respect of which the exemption has been
    granted; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the short title of the exemption order and the
    identifier set out in the exemption order.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The label must be securely applied to the windshield
  or a side window of the vehicle. However, in the case of a vehicle
  without a windshield or side windows or with windshield or side window
  surfaces that are too small for the label to be applied, the label
  must be securely applied in a readily accessible location and in such
  a manner that it is easily readable from outside the vehicle without
  moving any part of the vehicle.

::: HistoricalNote

- **[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  a company wishes, on the expiration of the period of an exemption
  referred to in subsection 13(3), to obtain a new exemption, the
  company shall submit, in writing, to the Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information required pursuant to that
    subsection; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a statement of the total number of vehicles sold in
    Canada under the expiring exemption.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a company wishes, on the expiration of the
  period of an exemption referred to in subsection 13(4), to obtain a
  new exemption, the company shall submit, in writing, to the Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information required pursuant to that
    subsection; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a statement of the total number of vehicles sold in
    Canada under the expiring exemption.

## [Notice of Defect]{.HTitleText1} {#h-478981 .Part}

- **[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of subsection 10(1) of the Act, a person, other than the
  first retail purchaser, who obtained a vehicle from a company is a
  prescribed person.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A notice of defect that is required to be given under
  subsection 10(1) of the Act shall be in writing, in either paper or
  electronic form, and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a notice given to the Minister, be
    in either official language; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a notice given to the vehicle's
    current owner or a prescribed person

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} be in the person's official language of choice,
      if it is known, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} be in both official languages.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A company shall give the notice of defect to the
  vehicle's current owner and to the prescribed person as soon as
  feasible, but not later than 60 days after the day on which the
  company gives the notice of defect to the Minister.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The notice of defect that is given to the Minister
  shall contain the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the company's name and its contact information for
    the purpose of correspondence;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number, title or other identification that is
    assigned by the company to the notice;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for each vehicle that may contain the defect, its
    prescribed class, make, model and model year and any other
    information that is necessary to permit its identification;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the period during which the vehicles were
    manufactured;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the estimated number of vehicles that could
    potentially contain the defect;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the estimated percentage of the vehicles referred
    to in paragraph (e) that contain the defect;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a description of the nature of the defect,
    including the causes and contributing factors, if known, and a
    description of the location of the defect;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle systems or components that may be
    affected by the defect;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a chronology of the principal events that led to
    the determination of the existence of the defect;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} all relevant information, including a summary of
    warranty claims, field reports and service reports, with their dates
    of receipt, that the company used to determine the existence of the
    defect;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} a description of the safety risk to persons arising
    from the defect;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} a description of the corrective measures to be
    taken in respect of the defect and how they are to be implemented;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} any precautions that may be taken to minimize the
    safety risk until the corrective measures are implemented; and

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} the estimated date on which the notice of defect
    will be sent to the vehicle's current owner and the estimated date
    on which the notice of defect will be sent to a prescribed person.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The company is not required to provide the Minister
  with the information referred to in paragraphs (4)(i), (j), (l)
  and (m) if it is not available on the day on which the notice is given
  but shall provide that information as soon as it is available.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} A notice of defect that is given to the vehicle's
  current owner shall contain the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the company's name;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the make, model, model year and the vehicle
    identification number;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the following statements:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} "This notice is sent to you in accordance with
      the requirements of the [Motor Vehicle Safety
      Act](/eng/acts/M-10.01).", and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} "This is to inform you that your vehicle may
      contain a defect that could affect the safety of a person.";

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the number, title or other identification that is
    assigned by the company to the notice;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the motor vehicle safety recall number that is
    issued by the Department of Transport;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a description of the nature of the defect,
    including the causes, and a description of the location of the
    defect;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle systems or components that may be
    affected by the defect;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} operating conditions or other factors that may
    cause a malfunction of the vehicle system or component;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the warning signs, if any, of any malfunction that
    could arise as a result of the defect;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a description of the safety risk to persons arising
    from the defect;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the defect could cause a crash, if
    applicable;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} if the defect is not likely to cause a crash, the
    type of injury that may result from the defect;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} a description of the corrective measures to be
    taken in respect of the defect and how they are to be implemented,
    including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a general description of the work involved,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the estimated time required in order to take the
      corrective measures,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the company will cover the
      costs of the corrective measures or an estimate of the cost of the
      corrective measures to the vehicle's current owner, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} information identifying the persons who can
      implement the corrective measures;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} any precautions that may be taken to minimize the
    safety risk until the corrective measures are implemented; and

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} a statement indicating that if the vehicle's
    current owner has leased the vehicle, the owner shall send to the
    lessee a copy of the notice and any subsequent notice within 10
    working days after the day on which the notice is received.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} The company is not required to provide to the current
  owner the information referred to in paragraph (6)(m) if it is not
  available on the day on which the notice is given but shall provide
  that information

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} as soon as it is available; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at the same time as the company provides the
    information required under subsection 10.4(1) of the Act.

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} The words "SAFETY", "RECALL", "[RAPPEL]{.otherLang
  lang="fr"}" and "[SÉCURITÉ]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" are required to be
  clearly visible

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on the envelope, or through a window of the
    envelope, in upper case and in a font size that is larger than the
    one used for the recipient's address, if the notice of defect is
    given to the current owner in paper form; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the subject line of the communication, in upper
    case, if the notice of defect is given to the current owner in
    electronic form.

- [(9)]{.lawlabel} A notice of defect that is given to a prescribed
  person shall contain the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the company's name;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for each vehicle that may contain the defect, its
    make, model, model year and vehicle identification number and any
    other information that is necessary to permit its identification;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the number, title or other identification that is
    assigned by the company to the notice;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a description of the nature of the defect,
    including the causes, and a description of the location of the
    defect;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} operating conditions or other factors that may
    cause a malfunction of the vehicle system or component;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a description of the safety risk to persons arising
    from the defect;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a description of the corrective measures to be
    taken in respect of the defect and how they are to be implemented;
    and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} any precautions that may be taken to minimize the
    safety risk until the corrective measures are implemented.

- [(10)]{.lawlabel} The company is not required to provide to the
  prescribed person the vehicle identification number under paragraph
  (9)(b) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle identification number is published on
    the website that the company uses to communicate information
    directed at the Canadian market along with the number, title or
    other identification that is assigned by the company to the
    associated notice of defect; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the information in the notice of defect is
    published in accordance with section 15.05.

- [(11)]{.lawlabel} The company is not required to provide to the
  prescribed person the information referred to in paragraph (9)(g) if
  it is not available on the day on which the notice is given but shall
  provide that information as soon as it is available.

## [Notice of Non-compliance]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1190717 .Part}

- **[[15.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15.01
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of
  subsection 10.1(1) of the Act, a person, other than the first retail
  purchaser, who obtained a vehicle from a company is a prescribed
  person.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A notice of non-compliance that is required to be
  given under subsection 10.1(1) of the Act shall be in writing, in
  either paper or electronic form, and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a notice given to the Minister, be
    in either official language; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a notice given to the vehicle's
    current owner or a prescribed person,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} be in the person's official language of choice,
      if it is known, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} be in both official languages.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Unless the notice of non-compliance contains a
  statement under subparagraph (4)(j)(i), a company shall give the
  notice of non-compliance to the vehicle's current owner and to the
  prescribed person as soon as feasible, but not later than 60 days
  after the day on which the company gives the notice of non-compliance
  to the Minister.

- [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} If the notice of non-compliance contains a
  statement under subparagraph (4)(j)(i) and the Minister advises the
  company that the Minister has determined that the non-compliance is
  not inconsequential to safety, the company shall give the notice of
  non-compliance to the vehicle's current owner and to the prescribed
  person as soon as feasible after the day on which the company receives
  the Minister's determination, but not later than 60 days after that
  day.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A notice of non-compliance that is given to the
  Minister shall contain the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the company's name and its contact information for
    the purpose of correspondence;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number, title or other identification that is
    assigned by the company to the notice;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for each vehicle that may be non-compliant, its
    prescribed class, make, model and model year and any other
    information that is necessary to permit its identification;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the period during which the vehicles were
    manufactured;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the estimated number of vehicles that could
    potentially be non-compliant;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the estimated percentage of the vehicles referred
    to in paragraph (e) that are non-compliant;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a description of the non-compliance, including the
    applicable regulatory requirement, and the causes and contributing
    factors, if known;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle systems or components that may be
    affected by the non-compliance;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a chronology of the principal events that led to
    the determination of the non-compliance, including the test results,
    observations, inspections and any other relevant information;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} as the case may be,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the non-compliance is
      inconsequential to safety, including detailed information in
      support of the statement, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a description of the safety risk to persons
      arising from the non-compliance;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} a description of the corrective measures to be
    taken in respect of the non-compliance and how they are to be
    implemented;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} any precautions that may be taken to minimize the
    safety risk until the corrective measures are implemented; and

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} the estimated date on which the notice of
    non-compliance will be sent to the vehicle's current owner and the
    estimated date on which the notice of non-compliance will be sent to
    a prescribed person.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The company is not required to provide the Minister
  with the information referred to in paragraphs (4)(i), (k) and (l) if
  it is not available on the day on which the notice is given but shall
  provide that information as soon as it is available.

- [(5.1)]{.lawlabel} The company is not required to provide the Minister
  with the information referred to in paragraph (4)(m) if the notice of
  non-compliance contains a statement under subparagraph (4)(j)(i), but
  if the Minister advises the company that the Minister has determined
  that the non-compliance is not inconsequential to safety, the company
  shall provide that information within five working days after the day
  on which the company receives the Minister's determination.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} A notice of non-compliance that is given to the
  vehicle's current owner shall contain the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the company's name;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the make, model, model year and the vehicle
    identification number;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the following statements:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} "This notice is sent to you in accordance with
      the requirements of the [Motor Vehicle Safety
      Act](/eng/acts/M-10.01).", and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} "This is to inform you that your vehicle may be
      non-compliant with the requirements of the [Motor Vehicle Safety
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1038) and that the
      non-compliance could affect the safety of a person.";

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the number, title or other identification that is
    assigned by the company to the notice;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the motor vehicle safety recall number that is
    issued by the Department of Transport;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a description of the non-compliance, including the
    causes;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle systems or components that may be
    affected by the non-compliance;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} operating conditions or other factors that may
    cause a malfunction of the vehicle system or component;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the warning signs, if any, of the malfunction that
    could arise as a result of the non-compliance;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a description of the safety risk to persons arising
    from the non-compliance, if any;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the non-compliance could cause a
    crash, if applicable;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} if the non-compliance is not likely to cause a
    crash, the type of injury that may result from the non-compliance;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} a description of the corrective measures to be
    taken in respect of the non-compliance and how they are to be
    implemented, including,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a general description of the work involved,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the estimated time required in order to take the
      corrective measures,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the company will cover the
      costs of the corrective measures or an estimate of the cost of the
      corrective measures to the vehicle's current owner, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} information identifying the persons who can
      implement the corrective measures;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} any precautions that may be taken to minimize the
    safety risk until the corrective measures are implemented; and

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} a statement indicating that if the vehicle's
    current owner has leased the vehicle, the owner shall send to the
    lessee a copy of the notice and any subsequent notice within 10
    working days after the day on which the notice is received.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} The company is not required to provide to the current
  owner the information referred to in paragraph (6)(m) if it is not
  available on the day on which the notice is given but shall provide
  that information

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} as soon as it is available; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at the same time as the company provides the
    information required under subsection 10.4(1) of the Act.

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} The words "SAFETY", "RECALL", "[RAPPEL]{.otherLang
  lang="fr"}" and "[SÉCURITÉ]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" are required to be
  clearly visible

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on the envelope, or through a window of the
    envelope, in upper case and in a font size that is larger than the
    one used for the recipient's address, if the notice of
    non-compliance is given to the current owner in paper form; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the subject line of the communication, in upper
    case, if the notice of non-compliance is given to the current owner
    in electronic form.

- [(9)]{.lawlabel} A notice of non-compliance that is given to a
  prescribed person shall contain the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the company's name;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for each vehicle that may be non-compliant, its
    make, model, model year and vehicle identification number and any
    other information that is necessary to permit its identification;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the number, title or other identification that is
    assigned by the company to the notice;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a description of the non-compliance, including the
    causes;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} operating conditions or other factors that may
    cause a malfunction of the vehicle system or component;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a description of the safety risk to persons arising
    from the non-compliance, if any;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a description of the corrective measures to be
    taken in respect of the non-compliance and how they are to be
    implemented; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} any precautions that may be taken to minimize the
    safety risk until the corrective measures are implemented.

- [(10)]{.lawlabel} The company is not required to provide to the
  prescribed person the vehicle identification number under paragraph
  (9)(b) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle identification number is published on
    the website that the company uses to communicate information
    directed at the Canadian market along with the number, title or
    other identification that is assigned by the company to the
    associated notice of non-compliance; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the information in the notice of non-compliance is
    published in accordance with section 15.05.

- [(11)]{.lawlabel} The company is not required to provide to the
  prescribed person the information referred to in paragraph (9)(g) if
  it is not available on the day on which the notice is given but shall
  provide that information as soon as it is available.

## [Reports]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1190851 .Part}

- **[[15.02]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15.02
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Within five working days after the
  day on which a company begins sending a notice of defect or
  non-compliance to current owners, the company shall provide to the
  Minister a report containing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the notice;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a sample of the envelope used to mail the notice;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the company began sending
    notices;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the company finished, or expects
    to finish, sending the notices;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the number of vehicles that are subject to the
    notice; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle identification number for each vehicle
    that may contain the defect or may be non-compliant, unless that
    information is provided to the Minister under paragraph (3)(a) or is
    published in accordance with subsection 15(10) or 15.01(10).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(b), a company is not required
  to provide a sample of the envelope if it uses an envelope that has
  previously been provided as a sample to the Minister and the report
  includes the date on which the sample was provided.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Within five working days after the day on which a
  company begins sending a notice of defect or non-compliance to
  prescribed persons, the company shall provide to the Minister a report
  containing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the notice; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if no notices are sent to current owners, the
    number of vehicles that are subject to the notice.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} For a period of five years beginning on the 60th day
  after the day on which a company gives a notice to the Minister under
  subsection 10(1) or 10.1(1) of the Act, the company shall provide the
  Minister, within five working days after the day on which they are
  sent to their recipients, a copy of any of the following
  communications, with the date on which they were sent to their
  recipients:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} communications sent to more than one current owner
    relating to the defect or non-compliance; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} communications sent to more than one prescribed
    person relating to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the information set out in subsection 15(9) or
      15.01(9), and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the defect or non-compliance.

- **[[15.03]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15.03
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of section 10.2 of
  the Act, a company that gives a notice of defect or non-compliance to
  a current owner or a prescribed person shall provide the Minister with
  quarterly reports that contain the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the motor vehicle safety recall number issued by
    the Department of Transport;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number, title or other identification that is
    assigned by the company to the notice;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the number of vehicles that are subject to the
    notice, including the day on which the number was updated by the
    company; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the number of vehicles for which corrective
    measures have been taken, including vehicles that required only an
    inspection, and the day on which that number was determined by the
    company.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The company shall provide the Minister with quarterly
  reports, in accordance with the following schedule, for a period of
  two years beginning on the 60th day after the day on which the company
  gives a notice to the Minister under subsection 10(1) or 10.1(1) of
  the Act:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the first calendar quarter, from January 1
    through March 31, on or before April 30;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the second calendar quarter, from April 1
    through June 30, on or before July 30;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for the third calendar quarter, from July 1 through
    September 30, on or before October 30; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} for the fourth calendar quarter, from October 1
    through December 31, on or before January 30 of the following year.

## [Publication of Information]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1506110 .Part}

- **[[15.04]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15.04
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A company that gives a notice of
  defect or non-compliance to the Minister under subsection 10(1) or
  10.1(1) of the Act must publish the following information, in both
  official languages, on the website that the company uses to
  communicate information directed at the Canadian market:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the notice was given to the
    Minister;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the make, model and model year of the vehicle
    referred to in the notice;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the number, title or other identification that is
    assigned by the company to the notice;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the motor vehicle safety recall number that is
    issued by the Department of Transport;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a description of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the nature of the defect, including the causes of
      the defect, and its location, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the non-compliance, including the causes;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a description of the safety risk to persons arising
    from the defect or non-compliance;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the defect or non-compliance could
    cause a crash, if applicable;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} if the defect or non-compliance is not likely to
    cause a crash, the type of injury that may result from the defect or
    non-compliance;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a description of the corrective measures to be
    taken in respect of the defect or non-compliance and how they are to
    be implemented;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} any precautions that may be taken to minimize the
    safety risk until the corrective measures are implemented;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the parts and facilities that are
    necessary to correct the defect or non-compliance are available or,
    if they are not, the earliest date by which they are expected to be
    available;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, a statement indicating that
    information in respect of notices that are given before a specified
    date is not available on the website and how to obtain the
    information;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} on each web page displaying the information
    referred to in paragraphs (a) to (l), instructions on how to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} contact the company or an automobile dealership
      concerning any questions related to the notice,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} report a safety concern relating to a vehicle,
      and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} inform the company of a transfer of ownership
      of a vehicle or a change of address; and

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} on each web page displaying the information
    referred to in paragraphs (a) to (l), the date on which the
    information was last updated.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The company must publish the information referred to
  in subsection (1) as soon as feasible, but not later than 60 days
  after the day on which the company gives the notice to the Minister,
  and must update the information within seven days after the day on
  which there is any change to it.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), if the information referred
  to in paragraph (1)(d), (i) or (k) is not available on the day on
  which it is to be published, the company must publish the information
  within seven days after the day on which it becomes available.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The company must ensure that the information referred
  to in subsection (1) remains displayed on the website for at least 15
  years beginning on the day on which the company gives the notice of
  defect or non-compliance to the Minister.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The information referred to in subparagraphs
  (1)(m)(i) to (iii) may be made available by way of a hyperlink.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The company must ensure that the information referred
  to in subsection (1) is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} available free of charge;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} accessible without the website user having to
    register or enter any information; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} displayed either directly on the website's home
    page or made available by way of a hyperlink that contains the word
    "Recall" or "Recalls" and that is prominently displayed on that home
    page.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} A company is not required to meet the requirements of
  this section if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the requirements have been met by another company
    that manufactured, sold or imported the vehicle;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the company has been designated by the Minister
    before this section applies to it;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the company meets the requirements of section
    15.05; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the company does not have a website to communicate
    information directed at the Canadian market.

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} This section applies to a company beginning on the
  first anniversary of the day on which this section comes into force.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-274, s. 4](#1502401-1506147){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1502401-1506147.html"
  role="button"}
:::

## [Publication of Information by Designated Companies]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1506148 .Part}

- **[[15.05]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15.05
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A company that is designated by the
  Minister and that gives a notice of defect or non-compliance to the
  Minister under subsection 10(1) or 10.1(1) of the Act must publish on
  the website that the company uses to communicate information directed
  at the Canadian market the following information, in both official
  languages, and make it available by means of a vehicle identification
  number search tool:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the notice was given to the
    Minister;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the make, model and model year of the vehicle
    referred to in the notice;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the number, title or other identification that is
    assigned by the company to the notice;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the motor vehicle safety recall number that is
    issued by the Department of Transport;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the corrective measures in respect of the defect
    or non-compliance have not been implemented

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a description of

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the nature of the defect, including the causes
        of the defect, and its location, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the non-compliance, including the causes,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a description of the safety risk to persons
      arising from the defect or non-compliance,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the defect or non-compliance
      could cause a crash, if applicable,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} if the defect or non-compliance is not likely to
      cause a crash, the type of injury that may result from the defect
      or non-compliance,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a description of the corrective measures to be
      taken in respect of the defect or non-compliance and how they are
      to be implemented,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} any precautions that may be taken to minimize
      the safety risk until the corrective measures are implemented, and

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the parts and facilities that
      are necessary to correct the defect or non-compliance are
      available or, if they are not, the earliest date by which they are
      expected to be available;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if the corrective measures in respect of the defect
    or non-compliance have been implemented

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the information referred to in subparagraphs
      (e)(i) and (v), and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the corrective measures have
      been implemented;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, a statement indicating that
    information in respect of notices that are given before a specified
    date is not available on the website and how to obtain the
    information;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} on each web page displaying the information
    referred to in paragraphs (a) to (g), instructions on how to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} contact the company or an automobile dealership
      concerning any questions related to the notice,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} report a safety concern relating to a vehicle,
      and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} inform the company of a transfer of ownership
      of a vehicle or a change of address; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the information was last updated
    on each web page displaying the information referred to in
    paragraphs (a) to (g).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The information referred to in subparagraphs
  (1)(h)(i) to (iii) must also be displayed on the web page containing
  the search tool or be made available by way of a hyperlink from that
  page, unless that page is the website's home page.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The company must publish the information referred to
  in subsection (1) as soon as feasible, but not later than 60 days
  after the day on which the company gives the notice to the Minister,
  and must update the information within seven days after the day on
  which there is any change to it.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (3), if the information referred
  to in subparagraph (1)(e)(v) or (vii) is not available on the day on
  which it is to be published, the company must publish the information
  within seven days after the day on which it becomes available.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The company must ensure that the information referred
  to in subsection (1) remains displayed on the website for at least 15
  years beginning on the day on which the company gives the notice of
  defect or non-compliance to the Minister.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} If no notice of defect or non-compliance is
  associated with a vehicle identification number when a search is
  carried out on the website, the company must ensure that a statement
  to that effect, as well as the following information, is displayed:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the make, model and model year of the vehicle;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, a statement indicating that
    information in respect of notices that are given before a specified
    date is not available on the website and how to obtain the
    information;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} instructions on how to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} contact the company for more information,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} report a safety concern relating to a vehicle,
      and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} inform the company of a transfer of ownership
      of a vehicle or a change of address; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the information referred to in
    paragraph (a) or (b) was last updated.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} If the search tool does not recognize the vehicle
  identification number, the company must ensure that a statement to
  that effect, as well as instructions on how to contact the company for
  more information, is displayed.

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} The information referred to in subparagraphs
  (1)(h)(i) to (iii) and paragraph 6(c) may be made available by way of
  a hyperlink.

- [(9)]{.lawlabel} The company must ensure that the search tool is
  available either directly on the website's home page or by way of a
  hyperlink that contains the word "Recall" or "Recalls" and that is
  prominently displayed on that home page.

- [(10)]{.lawlabel} The company must ensure that the information
  referred to in subsections (1), (6) and (7) is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} available free of charge;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} accessible without the user having to register or
    enter any information; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} displayed before any other information on the
    search tool results page.

- [(11)]{.lawlabel} This section applies to a company beginning on the
  day that, in the eighteenth month after the month in which it is
  designated by the Minister, has the same calendar number as the day on
  which it is designated or, if that month has no day with that number,
  the last day of that eighteenth month, but this section does not apply
  if the requirements of this section have been met by another company
  that manufactured, sold or imported the vehicle.

:::

## [Technical Standards Documents]{.HTitleText1} {#h-479013 .Part}

- **[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of section 12 of the Act, every technical standards
  document, including all of the amendments to it, shall be published by
  the Department of Transport and have the main title Technical
  Standards Document, followed by an identification number, a
  descriptive title and the publication date.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2008-104, s. 5\]]{.Repealed}

## [Owner's Manual]{.HTitleText1} {#h-479022 .Part}

- **[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of paragraph 5(1)(f) of the Act, for each vehicle that a
  company imports into Canada before the vehicle is sold to the first
  retail purchaser and for each vehicle to which a company applies a
  compliance label, the company shall provide, in written, electronic or
  optical form, an owner's manual containing the information required by
  these Regulations relating to the operation of the vehicle.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The owner's manual shall be available in both
  official languages.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the owner's manual is available only in electronic
  or optical form, it shall be capable of being used inside the occupant
  compartment using a device installed in or supplied with the vehicle.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} As of September 1, 2020, the owner's manual shall
  contain instructions on how the owner can contact the Department of
  Transport in order to report a safety concern relating to a vehicle.

## [Department of Transport]{.scheduleTitleText}  [Motor Vehicle Safety Act (section 3)]{.scheduleTitleText}  [Motor Vehicle Safety Regulations (subsection 3(2))]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#department-of-transport-motor-vehicle-safety-act-section-3-motor-vehicle-safety-regulations-subsection-32 .scheduleLabel}
:::

### [Ministerial Authorization]{.HTitleText1} {#ministerial-authorization .SchedHeadL1}

Under the [Motor Vehicle Safety Act](/eng/acts/M-10.01) and the [Motor
Vehicle Safety Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1038),

\[company name and address\]

is authorized to apply the national safety mark to any vehicle of a
prescribed class referred to in section 4 of the [Motor Vehicle Safety
Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1038), on the condition that
the vehicle conforms to all of the applicable Canada Motor Vehicle
Safety Standards.

The national safety mark must be applied at the following premises:

\[address of the premises\]

This ministerial authorization (number ![blank
line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.Leader-solid}) expires on ![blank
line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.Leader-solid}

Issued in Ottawa on ![blank line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.Leader-solid},
20![blank line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.Leader-solid}

::: FlushLeft
![blank line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.Leader-solid}
:::

::: FlushLeft
for the Minister of Transport
:::

### [PART I]{.HLabel1}[[\[Repealed, SOR/2007-180, s. 5\]]{.Repealed}]{.HTitleText1} {#part-irepealed-sor2007-180-s.-5 .SchedHeadL1}

### [PART II]{.HLabel1} {#part-ii .SchedHeadL1}

#### [Controls, Tell-tales, Indicators and Sources of Illumination (Standard 101)]{.HTitleText2} {#controls-tell-tales-indicators-and-sources-of-illumination-standard-101 .SchedHeadL2}

##### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText3} {#interpretation .SchedHeadL3}

- - **101** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section,
    [[control]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} has the same meaning as in
    Technical Standards Document No. 101, Controls, Tell-tales,
    Indicators and Sources of Illumination (TSD 101).

  ##### [General]{.HTitleText3} {#general .SchedHeadL3}

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every vehicle that is required by section 5 of
    these Regulations to conform to the standards set out in this
    section shall, in respect of the controls, tell-tales, indicators
    and sources of illumination that are fitted in the occupant
    compartment, conform to the requirements of TSD 101, as amended from
    time to time.

  ##### [Technical Standards Document No. 101]{.HTitleText3} {#technical-standards-document-no.-101 .SchedHeadL3}

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite S5.2.1 of TSD 101,

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the left turn signal and the right turn signal
      each have their own control or tell-tale, the arrows in the symbol
      required for the turn signals control or tell-tale may be
      disassociated and each arrow may be used separately as a distinct
      symbol;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the left turn signal and the right turn signal
      each have their own tell-tale and the arrows in the symbol
      required for the turn signals tell-tale are disassociated so that
      each arrow is used separately as a distinct symbol, the
      simultaneous flashing of the left and right turn signal tell-tales
      may be used as the hazard warning signal tell-tale;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the identification of a control set out below is
      not required if the control is combined with the master lighting
      switch:

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the control for the tail lamps, parking lamps,
        licence plate lamps, side marker lamps, identification lamps and
        clearance lamps, and

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the headlamp lower beam control;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if a single tell-tale is used to indicate more
      than one brake system condition, only the symbol required for the
      brake system malfunction shall be used;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the identification of a control set out below is
      not required if the control is an integral part of the key-locking
      system of the vehicle:

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the engine start control, and

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the engine stop control;

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the identification required for the drive
      position of the automatic transmission control may be replaced by
      a letter, a number, a combination of letters and numbers, or any
      symbol that is not set out in column 2 of the table to this
      section;

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the symbol required for the engine start control
      may be replaced by the word "start";

    - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the symbol required for the engine stop control
      may be replaced by the word "stop";

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the symbol required for the electronic stability
      control system malfunction tell-tale may be replaced by the
      abbreviation "ESC";

    - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the symbol required for the electronic stability
      control system off control and tell-tale may be replaced by the
      abbreviation "ESC OFF"; and

    - [(k)]{.lawlabel} until September 1, 2019, the symbol required for
      the passenger air bag deactivated control and tell-tale may be
      replaced by the words "passenger air bag off" or "pass air bag
      off".

  ##### [Speedometers and Odometers]{.HTitleText3} {#speedometers-and-odometers .SchedHeadL3}

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} A speedometer shall indicate the speed of the
    vehicle in kilometres per hour or in kilometres per hour and miles
    per hour. The unit or units of measurement shall be identified on
    the speedometer or at a location adjacent to it.

  - [(5)]{.lawlabel} A speedometer shall be illuminated whenever the
    vehicle's propulsion system and headlamps are activated, unless the
    headlamps are being flashed for signalling purposes or are being
    operated as daytime running lamps.

  - [(6)]{.lawlabel} An odometer or trip odometer shall indicate
    distances in kilometres or in miles. If the distances are indicated
    in miles, that unit of measurement shall be identified at a location
    adjacent to the odometer or trip odometer.

  ##### [Passenger Air Bag Deactivated Tell-tale]{.HTitleText3} {#passenger-air-bag-deactivated-tell-tale .SchedHeadL3}

  - [(7)]{.lawlabel} The tell-tale indicating that the passenger air bag
    has been deactivated shall be fitted in the interior of the vehicle

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} forward of and above the seating reference point
      of each front outboard designated seating position when the seat
      is in its forwardmost position; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in such a manner that the tell-tale, when alight,
      is visible to the driver and any front passenger when they are
      restrained by seat belts that are adjusted in accordance with the
      vehicle manufacturer's instructions.

  - [(8)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (7), the tell-tale indicating
    that the passenger air bag has been deactivated

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} shall not be fitted at or adjacent to a location
      that can serve for storage if an object stored at that location
      will obstruct the tell-tale from the view of the driver and any
      front passenger when they are restrained by seat belts that are
      adjusted in accordance with the vehicle manufacturer's
      instructions; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} shall not be fitted at a location where the
      tell-tale will not be completely visible to the driver when the
      driver is restrained by a seat belt that is adjusted in accordance
      with the vehicle manufacturer's instructions and a rearward-facing
      child restraint system or an infant restraint system is installed
      in the forwardmost right outboard designated seating position.

  ##### [Owner's Manual]{.HTitleText3} {#owners-manual .SchedHeadL3}

  - [(9)]{.lawlabel} The English and French versions of the owner's
    manual shall contain an explanation of every symbol, word,
    abbreviation or letter used to identify a control, tell-tale or
    indicator that is fitted in the vehicle and is required to be
    identified under this section.

  ##### [Transitional Provision]{.HTitleText3} {#transitional-provision-1 .SchedHeadL3}

  - [(10)]{.lawlabel} Until September 1, 2019, a vehicle referred to in
    subsection (2) may conform to the requirements of this section as it
    read on the day before the day on which this subsection came into
    force.

  - []{.lawlabel}

#### [Transmission Control Functions (Standard 102)]{.HTitleText2} {#transmission-control-functions-standard-102 .SchedHeadL2}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/97-421, s. 3

\]
:::

- - **102** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any automatic transmission fitted on a
    vehicle shall have

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} its control positions in such a sequence that

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} movement between any forward and reverse drive
        position may be made only through a neutral position,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a steering-column-mounted
        control, movement from the neutral position to any forward drive
        position may be made in a clockwise direction only, and

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the park position, if included in the
        sequence, is located at the end of the sequence adjacent to the
        reverse drive position; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} one forward drive position that, in vehicles
      having more than one forward transmission gear ratio, provides a
      greater degree of engine braking than the highest speed
      transmission ratio at speeds below 40 km/h.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} On any vehicle equipped with an automatic
    transmission, a motor used for the vehicle's propulsion must not be
    started by setting the ignition switch to the position used to start
    the motor if the transmission control is in a forward or reverse
    drive position.

  - [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} If a passenger car, multi-purpose passenger
    vehicle, truck or three-wheeled vehicle has a GVWR of 4 536 kg or
    less and a transmission control sequence that includes a park
    position, the transmission control must not be capable of shifting
    from the park position to the forward or reverse drive position ---
    while a motor used for the vehicle's propulsion is in use --- unless
    the service brake pedal is depressed or the service brakes are
    otherwise engaged.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (5), where the transmission
    control sequence includes a park position, the identification of the
    transmission control positions and the position selected shall be
    displayed in at least a single location in view of the driver when

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the ignition switch is set to the position where
      the transmission can be shifted; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the transmission control is not in the park
      position.

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (5), if the transmission
    control sequence does not include a park position, the
    identification of the transmission control positions and the
    position selected shall be displayed in at least a single location
    in view of the driver when the ignition switch is set to the
    position where the engine is capable of operation.

  - [(5)]{.lawlabel} The identification of transmission control
    positions need not be displayed when the ignition switch is set to
    the position used to start the vehicle.

  - [(6)]{.lawlabel} The identification of the transmission control
    positions and sequence, on vehicles equipped with a manual
    transmission, shall be permanently displayed in view of the driver.

  - [(7)]{.lawlabel} If a passenger car, multi-purpose passenger
    vehicle, truck or three-wheeled vehicle has a GVWR of 4 536 kg or
    less and is equipped with a manual transmission, a motor used for
    the vehicle's propulsion must not be started by setting the ignition
    switch to the position used to start the motor unless the clutch
    pedal is depressed or the drive train is otherwise disengaged.

  - [(8)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[drive
    train]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the components that transfer
    motive power from the motor to the drive wheels.

  ::: HistoricalNote
  - SOR/95-164, s. 4
  - SOR/97-421, ss. 4, 22(F)
  - SOR/2003-189, s. 1
  - SOR/2003-272, s. 34
  - SOR/2006-94, s. 4(E)
  - SOR/2007-246, s. 2
  :::

#### [Windshield Defrosting and Defogging]{.HTitleText2} {#windshield-defrosting-and-defogging .SchedHeadL2}

- - **103** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In this section,

    [[critical area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[critical area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means area C as referred
        to in section 104 of this Schedule; ([zone
        critique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

    [[defog]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[defog]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means to remove moisture from the
        inside surface of the glass; ([désembuer]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

    [[defrost]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[defrost]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means to melt frost or ice on
        the inside or outside surface of the
        glass; ([dégivrer]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

    [[entire windshield]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[entire windshield]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means area A as
        referred to in section 104 of this Schedule; ([pare-brise tout
        entier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

    [[road load]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[road load]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the power output
        required to move the vehicle at the curb mass plus 180 kg on
        level, clean, dry, smooth portland cement concrete pavement or
        other surface with an equivalent coefficient of surface friction
        at a specified speed through still air at 20°C and a standard
        barometric pressure of 101.3 kPa, and includes driveline
        friction, rolling friction and air resistance. ([charge de
        route]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2.1), every vehicle shall be
    equipped with a windshield defrosting and defogging system.

  - [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} A three-wheeled vehicle that is equipped with a
    windshield shall be equipped with a windshield defrosting and
    defogging system.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a passenger car or a three-wheeled
    vehicle, the windshield defrosting and defogging system shall

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} meet the requirements of section 3 of SAE
      Recommended Practice J902 Passenger Car Windshield Defrosting
      Systems, (August 1964), when tested in accordance with
      paragraph (b) except that the areas referred to in that section as
      "critical area" and "entire windshield" shall be as referred to in
      subsection (1) of this section; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be tested in accordance with such of the portions
      of paragraphs 4.1 to 4.4.7 of SAE Recommended Practice J902,
      (August 1964), or SAE Recommended Practice J902a, (March 1967), as
      are applicable to that system.

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite the testing requirements set out in
    subsection (3) for the windshield defrosting and defogging system of
    a passenger car or three-wheeled vehicle,

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a passenger car or three-wheeled
      vehicle equipped with a heating system other than a heat exchanger
      type that uses the engine's coolant as a means to supply the heat
      to the heat exchanger, the procedure specified by the vehicle's
      manufacturer for cold weather starting shall be followed during
      the entire test period, except that the use of a power or heat
      source external to the vehicle is not permitted;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of all other passenger cars and
      three-wheeled vehicles,

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} during the entire test period, the engine speed
        shall not exceed 1,500 revolutions per minute in neutral gear or
        the engine speed and load shall not exceed the speed and load at
        40 km/h (25 miles per hour) in the manufacturer's recommended
        gear with road load, or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} during the first 5 minutes of the test period,
        the warm-up procedure recommended by the vehicle's manufacturer
        for cold weather starting shall be followed and during the last
        35 minutes of the test period, the procedure referred to in
        subparagraph (i) shall be followed;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a room air change of 90 times per hour is not
      required;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the windshield wipers may be used during the test
      if they are operated without manual assistance;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} one or two windows may be open a total of 25 mm;

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the defroster blower may be turned on at any
      time;

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the wind velocity is at any level from 0 to
      3 km/h; and

    - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the test chamber temperature and the wind
      velocity shall be measured after the engine has been started, at
      the forwardmost point of the vehicle or a point 91.4 cm (36
      inches) from the base of the windshield, whichever is farther
      forward, at a level halfway between the top and the bottom of the
      windshield on the vehicle centreline.

  ::: HistoricalNote
  - SOR/97-264, s. 1
  - SOR/2003-272, s. 8
  - SOR/2008-104, s. 9
  :::

#### [Windshield Wiping and Washing System]{.HTitleText2} {#windshield-wiping-and-washing-system .SchedHeadL2}

- - **104** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In this section,

    [[areas A, B and C]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[areas A, B and C]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the areas
        referred to in Column I of Tables I, II, III and IV to this
        section when established as shown in Figures 1 and 2 of SAE
        Recommended Practice J903a Passenger Car Windshield Wiper
        Systems, (May 1966), using the angles specified in Columns III
        to VI of the above Tables; ([zones A, B et C]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

    [[daylight opening]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[daylight opening]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the maximum
        unobstructed opening through the glazing surface as defined in
        paragraph 2.3.12 of Section E, Ground Vehicle Practice, SAE
        Aerospace-Automotive Drawing Standards, (September
        1963); ([ouverture de jour]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

    [[glazing surface reference line]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[glazing surface reference line]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
        intersection of the glazing surface and a horizontal plane
        635 mm above the seating reference point, as shown in Figure 1
        of SAE Recommended Practice J903a (May 1966); ([ligne de
        référence de la surface vitrée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

    [[overall width]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[overall width]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the maximum overall
        body width dimension W116 as defined in Section E, Ground
        Vehicle Practice, SAE Aerospace-Automotive Drawing Standards,
        (September 1963); ([largeur hors tout]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

    [[plan view reference line]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[plan view reference line]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means,

        - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of vehicles with a bench type
          front seat, a line parallel to the longitudinal centreline of
          the vehicle and outboard of the centre of the steering wheel
          by a distance equal to 0.15 times the difference between
          one-half the shoulder room dimension and the steering wheel
          centre offset, as shown in Figure 2 of SAE Recommended
          Practice J903a, (May 1966); and

        - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of vehicles with individual front
          seats,

          - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a line parallel to the longitudinal
            centreline of the vehicle which passes through the centre of
            the driver's designated seating position, or

          - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a line parallel to the longitudinal
            centreline of the vehicle located so that the geometric
            centre of the 95 per cent eye range contour is positioned on
            the longitudinal centreline of the driver's designated
            seating position; ([ligne de référence
            longitudinale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

    [[shoulder room dimension]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[shoulder room dimension]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the front
        shoulder room dimension W3 as defined in Section E, Ground
        Vehicle Practice, SAE Aerospace-Automotive Drawing Standards,
        (September 1963); ([espace d'épaules]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

    [[95 per cent eye range contour]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[95 per cent eye range contour]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
        95th percentile tangential cut-off specified in SAE Recommended
        Practice J941a Passenger Car Driver's Eye Range, (August
        1967). ([95 pour cent du contour de portée
        visuelle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, the expressions
    "manikin H-point", "manikin H-point with seat in rearmost position",
    and "H-point" used in an SAE Standard or SAE Recommended Practice
    mean "seating reference point".

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3.1), every vehicle shall
    have a power-driven windshield wiping system that has at least two
    frequencies or speeds and that has, irrespective of engine speed and
    engine load,

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} one frequency or speed of at least 45 cycles per
      minute;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a difference of at least 15 cycles per minute
      between the highest frequency or speed and one of the lower
      frequencies or speeds; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the lower frequency or speed referred to in
      paragraph (b) equal to at least 20 cycles per minute.

  - [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} A three-wheeled vehicle that is equipped with a
    windshield shall have a windshield wiping system that conforms to
    the requirements of subsection (3).

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} Compliance with subsection (3) shall be
    demonstrated by testing under the conditions specified in sections
    4.1.1 and 4.1.2 of SAE Recommended Practice J903a, (May 1966).

  - [(5)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a passenger car or a three-wheeled
    vehicle, the windshield wiping system, when tested wet in accordance
    with SAE Recommended Practice J903a (May 1966), shall wipe the
    percentage of areas A, B and C of the windshield that

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is specified in Column II of whichever of Tables
      I, II, III or IV to this section is applicable; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is within the area bounded by a perimeter line on
      the glazing surface 25 mm from the edge of the daylight opening.

  - [(6)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (7), every vehicle shall have
    a windshield washing system that meets the requirements of SAE
    Recommended Practice J942, Passenger Car Windshield Washer Systems
    (November 1965), except that the words "the effective wipe pattern
    defined in SAE J903, paragraph 3.1.2" in paragraph 3.1 of SAE
    Recommended Practice J942 shall be replaced

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a passenger car or a three-wheeled
      vehicle, by the words "the areas established in accordance with
      the definition of areas A, B and C in subsection (1) of Canada
      Motor Vehicle Safety Standard No. 104"; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a multi-purpose passenger vehicle,
      truck or bus, by the words "the pattern designed by the
      manufacturer for the windshield wiping system on the exterior
      surface of the windshield glazing".

  - [(7)]{.lawlabel} A three-wheeled vehicle that is equipped with a
    windshield shall have a windshield washing system that meets the
    requirements of subsection (6).

  - []{.lawlabel}

#### [Hydraulic and Electric Brake Systems (Standard 105)]{.HTitleText2} {#hydraulic-and-electric-brake-systems-standard-105 .SchedHeadL2}

- - **105** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 135 of this Schedule,
    every multi-purpose passenger vehicle, truck and bus shall conform
    to the requirements of Technical Standards Document No. 105,
    Hydraulic and Electric Brake Systems (TSD 105), as amended from time
    to time.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} An indicator lamp referred to in S5.3 of TSD 105
    shall, when activated due to a condition set out in S5.3.1 of TSD
    105, display the identification symbol set out in the table to
    section 101 of this Schedule that corresponds to that condition, but
    if the vehicle is fitted with a single common indicator lamp, the
    lamp shall display the identification symbol for a brake system
    malfunction set out in the table to section 101 of this Schedule.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} The statement set out in S5.4.3 of TSD 105 may be
    replaced by another statement to the same effect.

  - [(4) to (6)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2015-24, s. 4\]]{.Repealed}

  ::: HistoricalNote
  - SOR/79-374, s. 3

#### [Brake Hoses (Standard 106)]{.HTitleText2} {#brake-hoses-standard-106 .SchedHeadL2}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/93-561, s. 3
- SOR/2011-238, s. 1

\]
:::

- - **106** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every brake hose, brake hose assembly and
    brake hose end fitting with which a passenger car, multi-purpose
    passenger vehicle, truck, bus, three-wheeled vehicle, motorcycle,
    trailer, and trailer converter dolly are equipped shall conform to
    the requirements of Technical Standards Document No. 106, Brake
    Hoses (TSD 106), as amended from time to time.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} A reference in TSD 106 to a standard published by
    ASTM that is set out in column 1 of the table to this subsection may
    be read as a reference to the standard set out opposite that
    standard in column 2 of the table.
