- **902** [\[Repealed, SOR/95-147, s. 11\]]{.Repealed}

#### [C-dolly Specifications]{.HTitleText2} {#c-dolly-specifications .SchedHeadL2}

- - **903** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every C-dolly shall be equipped with

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an automotive-type or a turntable-type
      self-steering mechanism;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a means to return the wheels to within one degree
      of the straight-ahead position whenever any steering force applied
      to the wheels is removed;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a device that automatically locks the steering
      mechanism in the straight-ahead position in the event of a failure
      of the means referred to in paragraph (b); and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a device

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that includes a switch by means of which the
        driver can, while seated in the normal driving position in the
        cab of the truck towing the C-dolly, lock the steering mechanism
        in the straight-ahead position, or unlock it,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} that locks the steering mechanism in the
        straight-ahead position in the event that the control for the
        device fails or the power supply to the device terminates, and

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} that includes, in both official languages,
        the information specified in subparagraphs (12)(b)(i) and (ii)
        on the switch referred in subparagraph (i) or on a label affixed
        near that switch.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every C-dolly shall have a GAWR of 9 100 kg.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} The longitudinal distance on a C-dolly, measured on
    the longitudinal centre-line from a point aligned with the centre of
    the coupling to a point aligned with the centre of the axle, shall
    be no greater than 2 m.

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where the coupling on a C-dolly consists of a
    single component, that coupling shall have

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a static load rating of

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 800 kN in the longitudinal direction, and

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} 80 kN in the lateral direction; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a static moment rating of 76.2 kN.m about the
      longitudinal axis of the C-dolly.

  - [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where the coupling on a C-dolly consists of two
    metal eyes, the two eyes shall

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be separated laterally by a distance of 762 mm ±
      2 mm;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be equidistant from the longitudinal centre-line
      of the C-dolly;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} have a mounting height of 900 mm ± 10 mm, when
      the C-dolly is not loaded; and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} each have a static load rating of

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 400 kN in the longitudinal direction,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} 100 kN in the vertical direction, and

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} 40 kN in the lateral direction.

  - [(6)]{.lawlabel} Where a C-dolly is tested in accordance with the
    Motor Vehicle Safety Test Methods, Section 903 - C-dolly (October 1,
    1992), published by the Department of Transport,

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the axis of rotation of a turntable-type steering
      mechanism shall not deviate from the vertical by more than 0.5
      degree;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the kingpin axes of an automotive-type steering
      mechanism shall not deviate from the vertical by more than 0.5
      degree;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the steering mechanism shall keep the wheels
      within one degree of the straight-ahead position when the steering
      mechanism is subjected to

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a lateral force of 22 320 N that is applied to
        a point located 50 mm behind the centre of the tire contact
        patch, or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a longitudinal force of 8 930 N that is
        applied through the centre of one of the tires in the case of a
        C-dolly that is equipped with one tire on each side, and through
        the centre of one of the dual tires and wheel assemblies in the
        case of a C-dolly that is equipped with two tires on each side;
        and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the steering mechanism shall not permit the
      wheels to move beyond one degree from the straight-ahead position
      unless the steering mechanism is subjected to a force exceeding
      the force referred to in subparagraph (c)(i) or (ii).

  - [(7)]{.lawlabel} Where a C-dolly is equipped with a pressurized
    hydraulic or pneumatic system that provides the means referred to in
    paragraph (1)(b), the system shall

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be independent of the brake system of the
      C-dolly;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be fitted with a pressure gauge that indicates

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the minimum pressure required to meet the
        requirements of paragraphs (1)(b) and (6)(c) and (d), and

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the amount of pressure in the system; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} not permit any adjustment of the steering force.

  - [(8)]{.lawlabel} Every C-dolly referred to in subsection (7) shall
    bear a label, in both official languages, that states the minimum
    pressure referred to in paragraph (7)(b).

  - [(9)]{.lawlabel} Every pressure gauge referred to in subsection (7)
    and every manual valve and switch, if any, that forms part of a
    system referred to in that subsection, and every label referred to
    in subsection (8), shall be protected from adverse weather
    conditions.

  - [(10)]{.lawlabel} Every C-dolly that is subjected to a torsion test
    conducted in accordance with the Motor Vehicle Safety Test Methods,
    Section 903 - C-dolly (October 1, 1992), published by the Department
    of Transport, shall have a torsional stiffness of at least
    3 000 N.m/degree with respect to the longitudinal direction, and
    shall be capable of sustaining a torque of at least 45 000 N.m in
    either direction without incurring a residual deformation that
    exceeds 0.5 degree.

  - [(11)]{.lawlabel} Where a C-dolly is equipped with a device that is
    intended to lock the steering mechanism automatically according to
    the speed of the C-dolly, that device shall

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} lock the steering mechanism in the straight-ahead
      position when the C-dolly is travelling at a speed of 60 km/h and
      keep the steering mechanism locked when the C-dolly is travelling
      at a speed greater than 60 km/h; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} unlock the steering mechanism when the C-dolly is
      travelling at a speed of 50 km/h and keep the steering mechanism
      unlocked when the C-dolly is travelling at a speed less than
      50 km/h.

  - [(12)]{.lawlabel} Every C-dolly shall

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} have a flat surface that is horizontal when the
      C-dolly is not loaded and when the centre of the coupling on the
      C-dolly is held at the mounting height in accordance with the
      manufacturer's design; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} bear a label, in both official languages, that
      states

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that the steering mechanism should be locked
        when the C-dolly is travelling at a speed of 60 km/h or more on
        any slippery or unpaved road, and that the steering mechanism
        should be unlocked when the C-dolly is travelling at a speed of
        50 km/h or less, and

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} that the C-dolly shall not be attached to a
        straight truck.

  - [(13)]{.lawlabel} The flat surface referred to in paragraph (12)(a)
    shall be

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at least 30 cm in length, measured
      longitudinally;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at least 5 cm in width, measured laterally;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} accessible from both sides of the C-dolly; and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} within 50 cm of the coupling of the C-dolly.

  ::: HistoricalNote
  - SOR/93-146, s. 4
  - SOR/2008-104, s. 22
  :::

#### [C-dolly Hitch Requirements]{.HTitleText2} {#c-dolly-hitch-requirements .SchedHeadL2}

- - **904** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every trailer that is designed to tow a
    C-dolly shall be equipped with a coupling to connect the trailer to
    the C-dolly.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a coupling referred to in subsection (1)
    consists of a single component, the coupling shall have

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a static load rating of

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 800 kN in the longitudinal direction, and

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} 80 kN in the lateral direction; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a static moment rating of 76.2 kN.m about the
      longitudinal axis of the C-dolly.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where a coupling referred to in subsection (1)
    consists of two separate hitches, the hitches shall

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be separated laterally by a distance of 762 mm ±
      2 mm;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be equidistant from the longitudinal centre-line
      of the trailer;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} have a mounting height of 900 mm ± 10 mm, when
      the trailer is not loaded; and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} each have a static load rating of

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 400 kN in the longitudinal direction,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} 100 kN in the vertical direction, and

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} 40 kN in the lateral direction.

  ::: HistoricalNote
  - SOR/93-146, s. 4
  - SOR/2008-104, s. 23
  :::

#### [Trailer Cargo Anchoring Devices (Standard 905)]{.HTitleText2} {#trailer-cargo-anchoring-devices-standard-905 .SchedHeadL2}

- - **905** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The definitions in this subsection apply in
    this section.

    [[bulkhead]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[bulkhead]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fixed or removable
        structure installed across the width of a trailer to restrict
        the movement of cargo during
        transport. ([cloison]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

    [[cargo anchoring device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[cargo anchoring device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fitting
        or attachment point on a trailer, other than a bulkhead or rub
        rail, to which a tie-down assembly is attached. ([dispositif
        d'ancrage des chargements]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

    [[rub rail]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[rub rail]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a protector strip
        extending horizontally along the sides of a trailer intended to
        provide protection from scuffing. ([bourrelet de
        protection]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} This section applies to every trailer that is
    designed to carry cargo, with a GVWR of 10 000 kg or more, a
    load-carrying mainframe and loading deck and no permanent sides or
    roof, such as a flatbed trailer, heavy hauler trailer, industrial
    trailer, lowbed trailer or drop-centre trailer.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every trailer shall, for the purpose of affixing
    cargo, be equipped with at least the number of cargo anchoring
    devices determined in accordance with the formula

    ::: {#481077 .Subsection}
    N = M / 2 000 kg

    where

    [N]{.dfn}
    :   is the minimum number of cargo anchoring devices, rounded up to
        the next even whole number; and

    [M]{.dfn}
    :   is the cargo carrying capacity of the trailer, calculated as the
        difference between its gross vehicle weight rating and its curb
        mass, expressed in kilograms.
    :::

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} The minimum number of cargo anchoring devices
    determined in accordance with subsection (3) shall, when tested in
    accordance with Test Method 905-Trailer Cargo Anchoring Devices
    (August 1998), withstand a force of at least 67 000 N applied in an
    upward vertical direction.

  ::: HistoricalNote
  - SOR/98-595, s. 2
  :::

#### [Snowmobile Trailers (Standard 906)]{.HTitleText2} {#snowmobile-trailers-standard-906 .SchedHeadL2}

- **906** Every snowmobile trailer shall be equipped with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} one or more tie-down cross bars located in such a
    manner as to be capable of clamping the skis of as many snowmobiles
    as the trailer is capable of carrying; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} tie-down points to which each snowmobile can be
    secured by ropes or straps and that are of sufficient strength to
    withstand a force of 2 224.11 N (500 pounds) applied separately from
    any direction to each tie-down point.

  ::: HistoricalNote
  - SOR/2003-359, s. 4
  :::

## [SCHEDULE V.1]{.scheduleLabel}[(Section 5)]{.OriginatingRef}[Noise Emissions (Standard 1106)]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-481094 .scheduleLabel}
:::

#### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#interpretation-2 .SchedHeadL2}

- **1** The term [[maximum rated engine speed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in
  Test Method 1106 --- Noise Emission Tests (August 2005) means the
  rotational speed of an engine in revolutions per minute (RPM) at which
  the maximum horsepower of the engine is attained.

#### [Exterior Sound Level]{.HTitleText2} {#exterior-sound-level .SchedHeadL2}

- **2** Subject to section 5, every vehicle, other than a motorcycle or
  a vehicle referred to in section 4, shall be so constructed that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} when tested in accordance with

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} section 3 of Test Method 1106 --- Noise Emission
      Tests (August 2005), the sound level does not exceed 83 dBA when a
      value of 2 dBA is subtracted from the reported vehicle sound level
      calculated in accordance with paragraph 3.3.3(c) of that Test
      Method, in the case of a bus with a GVWR of more than 4 536 kg,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} SAE Standard J1470, Measurement of Noise Emitted
      by Accelerating Highway Vehicles (June 1998), the sound level does
      not exceed 83 dBA when a value of 2 dBA is subtracted from the
      final reported value referred to in section 6.3 of that Standard,
      in the case of a bus, multi-purpose passenger vehicle, truck or
      chassis-cab with a GVWR of more than 2 722 kg and not more than
      4 536 kg,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} SAE Standard J1470, Measurement of Noise
      Emitted by Accelerating Highway Vehicles (June 1998), the sound
      level does not exceed 80 dBA when a value of 2 dBA is subtracted
      from the final reported value referred to in section 6.3 of that
      Standard, in the case of a passenger car regardless of its GVWR or
      any other vehicle with a GVWR of 2 722 kg or less,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} International Organization for Standardization
      standard ISO 362-1:2015, Measurement of noise emitted by
      accelerating road vehicles --- Engineering method --- Part 1: M
      and N categories, the sound level does not exceed 80 dBA when a
      value of 2 dBA is subtracted from the final result L~urban~,
      calculated in accordance with section 8.4 of that Standard, in the
      case of a bus, multi-purpose passenger vehicle, truck or
      chassis-cab with a GVWR of more than 2 722 kg and not more than
      4 536 kg, or

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} International Organization for Standardization
      standard ISO 362-1:2015, Measurement of noise emitted by
      accelerating road vehicles --- Engineering method --- Part 1: M
      and N categories, the sound level does not exceed 78 dBA when a
      value of 2 dBA is subtracted from the final result L~urban~
      calculated in accordance with section 8.4 of that Standard, in the
      case of a passenger car regardless of its GVWR or any other
      vehicle with a GVWR of 2 722 kg or less; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2021-83, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

- - **3** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every motorcycle shall be so constructed that

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} when tested in accordance with Annex 3 of United
      Nations Regulation No. 41, Uniform provisions concerning the
      approval of motor cycles with regard to noise, in the version in
      effect on June 25, 2008, as amended by any subsequent amendment in
      the 03 series of amendments, excluding any requirement in respect
      of the testing of stationary motorcycles contained in that Annex,
      the sound level referred to in paragraph 1.5.4 of that Annex does
      not exceed the limits specified in Annex 6 of that Regulation;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} when tested in accordance with Annex 3 and Annex
      7 of United Nations Regulation No. 41, Uniform provisions
      concerning the approval of motor cycles with regard to noise, in
      the version in effect on April 13, 2012, as amended by any
      subsequent amendment in the 04 series of amendments, excluding any
      requirement in respect of the testing of stationary motorcycles
      contained in Annex 3, the sound pressure level referred to in
      paragraph 1.4.6 of Annex 3 does not exceed the limits specified in
      Annex 6 of that Regulation; or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} when tested in accordance with

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} Appendix I-2 to subparts D and E, part 205,
        subchapter G, chapter I, title 40 of the United States Code of
        Federal Regulations, as amended by Vol. 45, No. 252 of the
        Federal Register of the United States published on December 31,
        1980, the reported noise level referred to in section (d)(3) of
        that Appendix does not exceed 70 dBA, in the case of a
        motorcycle with an engine displacement not exceeding 50 cm^3^
        and a maximum attainable speed of 48 km/h on a level paved
        surface, and

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} Appendix I-1 to subparts D and E, part 205,
        subchapter G, chapter I, title 40 of the United States Code of
        Federal Regulations, as amended by Vol. 45, No. 252 of the
        Federal Register of the United States published on December 31,
        1980, the reported noise level referred to in section (d)(3) of
        that Appendix in does not exceed 80 dBA, in any other case.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, the requirements
    of the United Nations Regulations referred to in paragraphs (1)(a)
    and (b) respecting the type-approval process do not apply.

- **4** Subject to section 5, every multi-purpose passenger vehicle,
  truck or chassis-cab with a GVWR of more than 4 536 kg shall be
  constructed so that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} when tested in accordance with the low speed sound
    emission test procedures set out in section 205.54-1, subpart B,
    part 205, subchapter G, chapter I, title 40 of the United States
    Code of Federal Regulations (revised as of December 5, 1977), the
    reported sound level does not exceed 80 dBA when calculated in
    accordance with section (c)(3)(iii) of section 205.54-1; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} when tested in accordance with International
    Organization for Standardization standard ISO 362-1:2015,
    Measurement of noise emitted by accelerating road vehicles ---
    Engineering method --- Part 1: M and N categories, the sound level
    does not exceed 81 dBA when a value of 2 dBA is subtracted from the
    final result L~urban~ calculated in accordance with section 8.4 of
    that Standard.

- - **5** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A vehicle referred to in sections 2 and 4 may
    be constructed so that

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} when tested in accordance with Annex 3 of United
      Nations Regulation No. 51, Uniform provisions concerning the
      approval of motor vehicles having at least four wheels with regard
      to their noise emissions, in the version in effect on March 9,
      2011, as amended by any subsequent amendment in the 02 series of
      amendments, excluding any requirement in respect of the testing of
      stationary vehicles contained in that Annex, the sound level
      referred to in paragraph 3.1.3 of that Annex does not exceed the
      limits specified in paragraph 6 of that Regulation; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} when tested in accordance with Annex 3 and Annex
      7 of United Nations Regulation No. 51, Uniform provisions
      concerning the approval of motor vehicles having at least four
      wheels with regard to their sound emissions, in the version in
      effect on January 20, 2016, as amended by any subsequent amendment
      in the 03 series of amendments, excluding any requirement in
      respect of the testing of stationary vehicles contained in Annex 3
      the final result L~urban~ referred to in paragraph 3.1.3 of Annex
      3 does not exceed the limits specified in paragraph 6 of that
      Regulation.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, the requirements
    of the United Nations Regulations referred to in subsection (1)
    respecting the type-approval process do not apply.

::: HistoricalNote

## [SCHEDULE VI]{.scheduleLabel}[(Section 5)]{.OriginatingRef} {#h-481128 .scheduleLabel}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- \[SOR/95-147, s. 13\]

\]
:::
::::

- **1200** [\[Repealed, SOR/96-360, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

#### [Snowmobiles (Standard 1201)]{.HTitleText2} {#snowmobiles-standard-1201 .SchedHeadL2}

- - **1201** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every snowmobile shall be constructed so
    that it conforms to the requirements set out in the Detailed
    Standards and Testing Specifications and Procedures, SSCC/11
    Supplement, published by the Snowmobile Safety and Certification
    Committee, Inc. (the Supplement), dated March, 2011, except that the
    following provisions do not apply:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the section entitled "Snowmobile Identification
      Numbering"; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the section entitled "Snowmobile Exhaust System
      Identification".

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} When a snowmobile is tested, the manufacturer may
    use, instead of the tests set out in the version of the Supplement,
    the tests set out in the version of the Supplement that is in effect
    on January 1 of the calendar year in which the snowmobile is
    manufactured.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, the word
    [[snowmobile]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} used in the Supplement has the
    same meaning as in subsection 2(1) of these Regulations.

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} The words "designed" and "designed to" used in the
    Supplement mean designed and constructed in such a manner so as to
    conform, under normal conditions of operation, to the performance
    requirements of the Supplement.

  - [(5)]{.lawlabel} The word "will" used in the Supplement shall be
    considered to create an obligation.

  - [(6)]{.lawlabel} The following shall be provided in both official
    languages:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} information to be provided on a label or placard
      as required by the Supplement; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any instructions that are provided with a
      snowmobile.

  - [(7)]{.lawlabel} Every snowmobile shall be equipped with headlamps
    that are on continuously when the engine of the snowmobile is
    operating.

  ::: HistoricalNote
  - SOR/87-497, s. 3
  - SOR/87-660, s. 6
  - SOR/96-360, s. 2
  - SOR/97-532, s. 4
  - SOR/2000-182, s. 9
  - SOR/2003-359, s. 5
  - SOR/2016-318, s. 15
  :::

- **1202** [\[Repealed, SOR/2016-318, s. 15\]]{.Repealed}
